theorem PEqualNP.Outside.L_meta_3807 : ∃ (x1 : ℕ), ∀ (x2 : ℕ) (x3 : SimpleGraph (Fin x2)) [x4 : DecidableRel x3.Adj], (∀ (x5 : Fin x2) (x6 : x3.Walk x5 x5), x6.IsCycle → (5 : ℕ) ≤ x6.length) → x3.edgeFinset.Nonempty → ∀ (x7 : PEqualNP.CircuitLowerBounds.Circuit), PEqualNP.TopForm.IsLeastAO x3 x7 → PEqualNP.TopForm.TopForm x3 x7 → (3 : ℕ) ≤ (PEqualNP.TopForm.junk x3 x7).card → (∀ x11 ∈ PEqualNP.TopForm.junk x3 x7, ∀ x13 ∈ PEqualNP.TopForm.junk x3 x7, ¬PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.InCone x2 x7 x11 x13) → {x12 ∈ PEqualNP.TopForm.Ustar x3 x7 | ∃ (x13 : Fin x2) (x14 : Fin x2), x12 = s(x13, x14) ∧ ∃ x15 ∈ PEqualNP.TopForm.tops x3 x7, ∃ x16 ∈ PEqualNP.TopForm.junk x3 x7, ∃ x17 ∈ PEqualNP.TopForm.junk x3 x7, x16 ≠ x17 ∧ PEqualNP.CleanedNonEdge.OrReaches x2 x7 (PEqualNP.TopForm.opnd x7 x15 (0 : Fin (2 : ℕ))) x16 ∧ PEqualNP.CleanedNonEdge.OrReaches x2 x7 (PEqualNP.TopForm.opnd x7 x15 (1 : Fin (2 : ℕ))) x17 ∧ PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.gateVal x2 x7 x16 (PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.pairInput x13 x14) = Bool.true ∧ PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.gateVal x2 x7 x17 (PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.pairInput x13 x14) = Bool.true}.card ≤ x1 * (x7.size + (1 : ℕ)) := by
  refine ⟨531,?_⟩
  intro n G inst girth _ C least top _ par
  classical
  have sq := codeg_of_girth girth
  have law : PEqualNP.Outside.L_meta_3807.PairCharge.AtomLaw G C (exc G C) := atomLawOf least.1 top par sq (exc G C) (fun lit abs => far_two lit abs)
  refine (countCases least.1 sq law _ ?_).trans ?_
  · intro e he
    obtain ⟨_,u,v,rfl,t,ht,g,hg,h,hh,_,r0,r1,gx,hx⟩ := Finset.mem_filter.mp he
    rcases edgeCover least.1 top par sq ht hg hh (orReaches_reach _ _ r0).1 (orReaches_reach _ _ r1).1 gx hx with heavy|same|light
    · exact Or.inl heavy
    · exact Or.inr (Or.inl (sameCase_of same))
    · exact Or.inr (Or.inr light)
  · omega


lean/PEqualNP/Outside/R_meta_1768_417.lean

theorem PEqualNP.Outside.R_meta_1768_417
    (support : ∀ (n : ℕ) (G : SimpleGraph (Fin n)) [DecidableRel G.Adj] (C : Circuit),
      IsLeastAO G C → G.support.ncard ≤ C.size + 1)
    (geometry : ∀ (n : ℕ) (G : SimpleGraph (Fin n)) [DecidableRel G.Adj] (C : Circuit),
      (∀ (v : Fin n) (p : G.Walk v v), p.IsCycle → 5 ≤ p.length) → IsLeastAO G C →
      ∀ (u u' v w : Fin n) (a a' : ℕ) (b b' : Fin 2), s(u,v) ∈ Ustar G C → s(u',v) ∈ Ustar G C →
        a ∈ tops G C → a' ∈ tops G C → u ∈ side C a b → u' ∈ side C a' b' →
        w ∈ side C a (1-b) → w ∈ side C a' (1-b') → u = u')
    (core : ∃ k : ℕ, ∀ (n : ℕ) (G : SimpleGraph (Fin n)) [DecidableRel G.Adj] (C : Circuit),
      (∀ (v : Fin n) (p : G.Walk v v), p.IsCycle → 5 ≤ p.length) →
      IsLeastAO G C → TopForm G C →
      ∀ (T : Finset (Sym2 (Fin n))) (u v : Sym2 (Fin n) → Fin n)
        (a g : Sym2 (Fin n) → ℕ) (b : Sym2 (Fin n) → Fin 2),
      (∀ e ∈ T, e ∈ Ustar G C ∧ e = s(u e, v e) ∧ a e ∈ tops G C ∧ g e ∈ junk G C ∧
        u e ∈ side C (a e) (b e) ∧ OrReaches n C (opnd C (a e) (1-b e)) (g e) ∧
        gateVal n C (g e) (pairInput (u e) (v e)) = true) →
      (∀ e ∈ T, ∀ f ∈ [fun z => (u z).val, fun z => (v z).val, fun z => 2*a z+(b z).val,g],
        3 ≤ (T.filter fun z => f z = f e).card) →
      (∀ (w : Fin n), ∀ e ∈ T, ∀ d ∈ T, v e = v d →
        w ∈ side C (a e) (1-b e) → w ∈ side C (a d) (1-b d) → e = d) →
      T.card ≤ k*(C.size+1)) :
    ∃ (x1 : ℕ), ∀ (x2 : ℕ) (x3 : SimpleGraph (Fin x2)) [x4 : DecidableRel x3.Adj] (x5 : PEqualNP.CircuitLowerBounds.Circuit), (∀ (x6 : Fin x2) (x7 : x3.Walk x6 x6), x7.IsCycle → (5 : ℕ) ≤ x7.length) → PEqualNP.TopForm.IsLeastAO x3 x5 → PEqualNP.TopForm.TopForm x3 x5 → {x9 ∈ PEqualNP.TopForm.Ustar x3 x5 | ∃ (x10 : Fin x2) (x11 : Fin x2), x9 = s(x10, x11) ∧ ∃ x12 ∈ PEqualNP.TopForm.tops x3 x5, ∃ (x13 : Fin (2 : ℕ)), ∃ x14 ∈ PEqualNP.TopForm.junk x3 x5, x10 ∈ PEqualNP.TopForm.side x5 x12 x13 ∧ PEqualNP.CleanedNonEdge.OrReaches x2 x5 (PEqualNP.TopForm.opnd x5 x12 ((1 : Fin (2 : ℕ)) - x13)) x14 ∧ PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.gateVal x2 x5 x14 (PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.pairInput x10 x11) = Bool.true}.card ≤ x1 * (x5.size + (1 : ℕ)) := by
  classical
  obtain ⟨k,hcore⟩ := core
  refine ⟨k+10,?_⟩
  intro n G inst C hcy hleast htop
  let S := (Ustar G C).filter (fun e => ∃ u v : Fin n, e = s(u,v) ∧
    ∃ a ∈ tops G C, ∃ b : Fin 2, ∃ g ∈ junk G C, u ∈ side C a b ∧
    OrReaches n C (opnd C a (1-b)) g ∧ gateVal n C g (pairInput u v) = true)
  change S.card ≤ (k+10)*(C.size+1)
  by_cases hne : S.Nonempty
  swap
  · simp [Finset.not_nonempty_iff_eq_empty.mp hne]
  obtain ⟨e₀,he₀⟩ := hne
  obtain ⟨u₀,_,_⟩ := (Finset.mem_filter.mp he₀).2
  have witnesses (e : Sym2 (Fin n)) : ∃ (u v : Fin n) (a g : ℕ) (b : Fin 2), e ∈ S →
      e ∈ Ustar G C ∧ e = s(u,v) ∧ a ∈ tops G C ∧ g ∈ junk G C ∧ u ∈ side C a b ∧
      OrReaches n C (opnd C a (1-b)) g ∧ gateVal n C g (pairInput u v) = true := by
    by_cases h : e ∈ S
    · obtain ⟨hs,u,v,he,a,ha,b,g,hg,hu,hr,hv⟩ := Finset.mem_filter.mp h
      exact ⟨u,v,a,g,b,fun _ => ⟨hs,he,ha,hg,hu,hr,hv⟩⟩
    · exact ⟨u₀,u₀,0,0,0,fun he => (h he).elim⟩
  choose u v a g b hw using witnesses
  let label : Fin 4 → Sym2 (Fin n) → ℕ :=
    ![fun e => (u e).val,fun e => (v e).val,fun e => 2*a e+(b e).val,g]
  let V := G.support.toFinset.image Fin.val
  let ranges : Fin 4 → Finset ℕ := ![V,V,Finset.range (2*C.size),Finset.range C.size]
  let R := (Finset.univ : Finset (Fin 4)).sigma ranges
  have cap : R.card ≤ 5*(C.size+1) := by
    have hV : V.card ≤ C.size+1 := by
      apply Finset.card_image_le.trans
      have he := (Set.ncard_eq_toFinset_card' G.support).symm
      rw [he]
      exact support n G C hleast
    change ((Finset.univ : Finset (Fin 4)).sigma ranges).card ≤ _
    rw [Finset.card_sigma,Fin.sum_univ_four]
    change V.card + V.card + (Finset.range (2*C.size)).card + (Finset.range C.size).card ≤ _
    simp only [Finset.card_range]
    omega
  have allowed (e) (he : e ∈ S) (j : Fin 4) : (⟨j,label j e⟩ : Sigma (fun _ : Fin 4 => ℕ)) ∈ R := by
    obtain ⟨hu,heq,ht,hj,_,_,_⟩ := hw e he
    have hAdj : G.Adj (u e) (v e) := by
      have hm := (Finset.mem_sdiff.mp hu).1
      rw [heq] at hm
      exact G.mem_edgeFinset.mp hm
    have hU : (u e).val ∈ V := Finset.mem_image.mpr ⟨u e,by simpa using (show u e ∈ G.support from ⟨_,hAdj⟩),rfl⟩
    have hV : (v e).val ∈ V := Finset.mem_image.mpr ⟨v e,by simpa using (show v e ∈ G.support from ⟨_,hAdj.symm⟩),rfl⟩
    have index {P : ℕ → Prop} {dec : DecidablePred P} {i : ℕ}
        (h : i ∈ @gatesWhere C P dec) : i < C.size := by
      exact Finset.mem_range.mp (Finset.mem_filter.mp (show i ∈ (Finset.range C.size).filter P from h)).1
    have hA : a e < C.size := index ht
    have hG : g e < C.size := index hj
    apply Finset.mem_sigma.mpr
    refine ⟨Finset.mem_univ _,?_⟩
    fin_cases j
    · exact hU
    · exact hV
    · apply Finset.mem_range.mpr
      change 2*a e + (b e).val < 2*C.size
      have := (b e).isLt
      omega
    · exact Finset.mem_range.mpr hG
  have peel (m : ℕ) : ∀ (P : Finset (Sigma (fun _ : Fin 4 => ℕ))) (A : Finset (Sym2 (Fin n))),
      P.card = m → (∀ e ∈ A, ∀ j, (⟨j,label j e⟩ : Sigma (fun _ : Fin 4 => ℕ)) ∈ P) →
      ∃ T ⊆ A, (∀ e ∈ T, ∀ j, 3 ≤ (T.filter fun z => label j z = label j e).card) ∧
        A.card ≤ T.card + 2*P.card := by
    induction m using Nat.strong_induction_on with
    | ind m ih =>
      intro P A hm hp
      by_cases h : ∀ e ∈ A, ∀ j, 3 ≤ (A.filter fun z => label j z = label j e).card
      · exact ⟨A,Finset.Subset.rfl,h,by omega⟩
      · push Not at h
        obtain ⟨e,he,j,hfew⟩ := h
        let B := A.filter fun z => label j z = label j e
        let r : Sigma (fun _ : Fin 4 => ℕ) := ⟨j,label j e⟩
        have hr : r ∈ P := hp e he j
        have hlt : (P.erase r).card < m := hm ▸ Finset.card_erase_lt_of_mem hr
        have rem (z) (hz : z ∈ A \ B) (i) : (⟨i,label i z⟩ : Sigma (fun _ : Fin 4 => ℕ)) ∈ P.erase r := by
          obtain ⟨hza,hzb⟩ := Finset.mem_sdiff.mp hz
          apply Finset.mem_erase.mpr
          refine ⟨?_,hp z hza i⟩
          intro eq
          have ij : i = j := congrArg Sigma.fst eq
          subst i
          have same : label j z = label j e := congrArg (fun p : Sigma (fun _ : Fin 4 => ℕ) => p.2) eq
          exact hzb (Finset.mem_filter.mpr ⟨hza,same⟩)
        obtain ⟨T,hTA,hT,hsize⟩ := ih _ hlt _ _ rfl rem
        refine ⟨T,hTA.trans Finset.sdiff_subset,hT,?_⟩
        have hsum := Finset.card_sdiff_add_card_eq_card (Finset.filter_subset (fun z => label j z = label j e) A)
        change (A \ B).card + B.card = A.card at hsum
        change B.card < 3 at hfew
        omega
  obtain ⟨T,hT,hRich,hpay⟩ := peel R.card R S rfl allowed
  have hBound := hcore n G C hcy hleast htop T u v a g b (fun e he => hw e (hT he)) (by
    intro e he f hf
    simp only [List.mem_cons,List.not_mem_nil,or_false] at hf
    rcases hf with rfl|rfl|rfl|rfl
    · exact hRich e he 0
    · exact hRich e he 1
    · exact hRich e he 2
    · exact hRich e he 3) (by
    intro w e he d hd hmate hw1 hw2
    obtain ⟨hU,heq,ha,_,hside,_,_⟩ := hw e (hT he)
    obtain ⟨hU',heq',ha',_,hside',_,_⟩ := hw d (hT hd)
    have hu : u e = u d := geometry n G C hcy hleast (u e) (u d) (v e) w (a e) (a d)
      (b e) (b d) (heq ▸ hU) (by rw [hmate,← heq']; exact hU') ha ha' hside hside' hw1 hw2
    rw [heq,heq',hu,hmate])
  rw [Nat.add_mul]
  omega


lean/PEqualNP/Outside/R_meta_606_3.lean

theorem PEqualNP.Outside.R_meta_606_3
    (normalize : ∀ (n : ℕ) (G : SimpleGraph (Fin n)) [DecidableRel G.Adj],
      (∀ (v : Fin n) (p : G.Walk v v), p.IsCycle → 5 ≤ p.length) → G.edgeFinset.Nonempty →
      ∀ D : Circuit, Monotone2 D →
      circuitFun n D = (fun x : Fin n → Bool => decide (∃ i j : Fin n, G.Adj i j ∧ x i = true ∧ x j = true)) →
      ∃ C : Circuit, IsLeastAO G C ∧ PEqualNP.TopForm.TopForm G C ∧ C.size ≤ D.size ∧
        (G.edgeFinset \ Ustar G C).card ≤ C.size + 1)
    (trace : ∀ (n : ℕ) (G : SimpleGraph (Fin n)) [DecidableRel G.Adj] (C : Circuit),
      IsLeastAO G C → PEqualNP.TopForm.TopForm G C → ∀ e ∈ Ustar G C,
      (∃ u v : Fin n, e = s(u, v) ∧ ∃ a ∈ tops G C, ∃ b : Fin 2, ∃ g ∈ junk G C,
        u ∈ side C a b ∧ OrReaches n C (opnd C a (1 - b)) g ∧
        gateVal n C g (pairInput u v) = true) ∨
      (∃ u v : Fin n, e = s(u, v) ∧ ∃ a ∈ tops G C, ∃ g ∈ junk G C, ∃ h ∈ junk G C,
        g ≠ h ∧ OrReaches n C (opnd C a 0) g ∧ OrReaches n C (opnd C a 1) h ∧
        gateVal n C g (pairInput u v) = true ∧ gateVal n C h (pairInput u v) = true))
    (single : ∃ k : ℕ, ∀ (n : ℕ) (G : SimpleGraph (Fin n)) [DecidableRel G.Adj] (C : Circuit),
      (∀ (v : Fin n) (p : G.Walk v v), p.IsCycle → 5 ≤ p.length) →
      IsLeastAO G C → PEqualNP.TopForm.TopForm G C →
      ((Ustar G C).filter (fun e => ∃ u v : Fin n, e = s(u, v) ∧
        ∃ a ∈ tops G C, ∃ b : Fin 2, ∃ g ∈ junk G C,
        u ∈ side C a b ∧ OrReaches n C (opnd C a (1 - b)) g ∧
        gateVal n C g (pairInput u v) = true)).card ≤ k * (C.size + 1))
    (double : ∃ k : ℕ, ∀ (n : ℕ) (G : SimpleGraph (Fin n)) [DecidableRel G.Adj] (C : Circuit),
      (∀ (v : Fin n) (p : G.Walk v v), p.IsCycle → 5 ≤ p.length) →
      IsLeastAO G C → PEqualNP.TopForm.TopForm G C →
      ((Ustar G C).filter (fun e =>
        (¬ ∃ u v : Fin n, e = s(u, v) ∧ ∃ a ∈ tops G C, ∃ b : Fin 2, ∃ g ∈ junk G C,
          u ∈ side C a b ∧ OrReaches n C (opnd C a (1 - b)) g ∧
          gateVal n C g (pairInput u v) = true) ∧
        (∃ u v : Fin n, e = s(u, v) ∧ ∃ a ∈ tops G C, ∃ g ∈ junk G C, ∃ h ∈ junk G C,
        g ≠ h ∧ OrReaches n C (opnd C a 0) g ∧ OrReaches n C (opnd C a 1) h ∧
        gateVal n C g (pairInput u v) = true ∧ gateVal n C h (pairInput u v) = true))).card ≤ k * (C.size + 1)) :
    ∃ c : ℕ, ∀ (n : ℕ) (G : SimpleGraph (Fin n)) [DecidableRel G.Adj],
      (∀ (v : Fin n) (p : G.Walk v v), p.IsCycle → 5 ≤ p.length) →
      ∀ D : PEqualNP.CircuitLowerBounds.Circuit, PEqualNP.MetaComplexity.Monotone2 D →
      PEqualNP.MetaComplexity.circuitFun n D = (fun x : Fin n → Bool => decide
        (∃ i j : Fin n, G.Adj i j ∧ x i = true ∧ x j = true)) →
      G.edgeFinset.card ≤ c * (D.size + 1) := by
  obtain ⟨k₁, h₁⟩ := single
  obtain ⟨k₂, h₂⟩ := double
  refine ⟨1 + k₁ + k₂, ?_⟩
  intro n G inst hG D hD hFun
  classical
  by_cases hE : G.edgeFinset.Nonempty
  · obtain ⟨C, hLeast, hTop, hSize, hCovered⟩ := normalize n G hG hE D hD hFun
    have hSingle := h₁ n G C hG hLeast hTop
    have hDouble := h₂ n G C hG hLeast hTop
    generalize hS : ((Ustar G C).filter (fun e => ∃ u v : Fin n, e = s(u, v) ∧
      ∃ a ∈ tops G C, ∃ b : Fin 2, ∃ g ∈ junk G C,
      u ∈ side C a b ∧ OrReaches n C (opnd C a (1 - b)) g ∧
      gateVal n C g (pairInput u v) = true)) = S at hSingle
    generalize hT : ((Ustar G C).filter (fun e =>
      (¬ ∃ u v : Fin n, e = s(u, v) ∧ ∃ a ∈ tops G C, ∃ b : Fin 2, ∃ g ∈ junk G C,
        u ∈ side C a b ∧ OrReaches n C (opnd C a (1 - b)) g ∧
        gateVal n C g (pairInput u v) = true) ∧
      (∃ u v : Fin n, e = s(u, v) ∧ ∃ a ∈ tops G C, ∃ g ∈ junk G C, ∃ h ∈ junk G C,
      g ≠ h ∧ OrReaches n C (opnd C a 0) g ∧ OrReaches n C (opnd C a 1) h ∧
      gateVal n C g (pairInput u v) = true ∧ gateVal n C h (pairInput u v) = true))) = T at hDouble
    have hSub : Ustar G C ⊆ S ∪ T := by
      intro e he
      by_cases hs : e ∈ S
      · exact Finset.mem_union_left _ hs
      · have hn : ¬ ∃ u v : Fin n, e = s(u, v) ∧ ∃ a ∈ tops G C, ∃ b : Fin 2, ∃ g ∈ junk G C,
          u ∈ side C a b ∧ OrReaches n C (opnd C a (1 - b)) g ∧
          gateVal n C g (pairInput u v) = true :=
          fun h => hs (hS ▸ Finset.mem_filter.mpr ⟨he, h⟩)
        have ht := (trace n G C hLeast hTop e he).resolve_left hn
        exact Finset.mem_union_right _ (hT ▸ Finset.mem_filter.mpr ⟨he, hn, ht⟩)
    have hResidual : (Ustar G C).card ≤ k₁ * (C.size + 1) + k₂ * (C.size + 1) :=
      (Finset.card_le_card hSub).trans ((Finset.card_union_le S T).trans (Nat.add_le_add hSingle hDouble))
    have hPartition := Finset.card_sdiff_add_card_eq_card
      (Finset.sdiff_subset : Ustar G C ⊆ G.edgeFinset)
    calc
      G.edgeFinset.card = (G.edgeFinset \ Ustar G C).card + (Ustar G C).card := hPartition.symm
      _ ≤ (C.size + 1) + (k₁ * (C.size + 1) + k₂ * (C.size + 1)) := Nat.add_le_add hCovered hResidual
      _ = (1 + k₁ + k₂) * (C.size + 1) := by simp [Nat.add_mul, Nat.add_assoc]
      _ ≤ (1 + k₁ + k₂) * (D.size + 1) := Nat.mul_le_mul_left _ (Nat.succ_le_succ hSize)
  · simp [Finset.not_nonempty_iff_eq_empty.mp hE]


lean/PEqualNP/Outside/L_meta_1878.lean

theorem PEqualNP.Outside.L_meta_1878 : ∃ (x1 : ℕ), ∀ (x2 : ℕ) (x3 : SimpleGraph (Fin x2)) [x4 : DecidableRel x3.Adj], (∀ (x5 : Fin x2) (x6 : x3.Walk x5 x5), x6.IsCycle → (5 : ℕ) ≤ x6.length) → x3.edgeFinset.Nonempty → ∀ (x7 : PEqualNP.CircuitLowerBounds.Circuit) (x8 : ℕ), PEqualNP.TopForm.IsLeastAO x3 x7 → PEqualNP.TopForm.TopForm x3 x7 → PEqualNP.TopForm.junk x3 x7 = {x8} → (PEqualNP.TopForm.Ustar x3 x7).card ≤ x1 * (x7.size + (1 : ℕ)) := by
  exact ⟨12, fun n G _ hg5 _ C g hL hT hj =>
    PEqualNP.Outside.L_meta_1878.main G C hg5 hL hT g hj⟩

theorem PEqualNP.Outside.L_meta_3805 : ∃ (x1 : ℕ), ∀ (x2 : ℕ) (x3 : SimpleGraph (Fin x2)) [x4 : DecidableRel x3.Adj], (∀ (x5 : Fin x2) (x6 : x3.Walk x5 x5), x6.IsCycle → (5 : ℕ) ≤ x6.length) → x3.edgeFinset.Nonempty → ∀ (x7 : PEqualNP.CircuitLowerBounds.Circuit), PEqualNP.TopForm.IsLeastAO x3 x7 → PEqualNP.TopForm.TopForm x3 x7 → (3 : ℕ) ≤ (PEqualNP.TopForm.junk x3 x7).card → (∀ x11 ∈ PEqualNP.TopForm.junk x3 x7, ∀ x13 ∈ PEqualNP.TopForm.junk x3 x7, ¬PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.InCone x2 x7 x11 x13) → {x12 ∈ PEqualNP.TopForm.Ustar x3 x7 | ∃ (x13 : Fin x2) (x14 : Fin x2), x12 = s(x13, x14) ∧ ∃ x15 ∈ PEqualNP.TopForm.tops x3 x7, ∃ (x16 : Fin (2 : ℕ)), ∃ x17 ∈ PEqualNP.TopForm.junk x3 x7, x13 ∈ PEqualNP.TopForm.side x7 x15 x16 ∧ PEqualNP.CleanedNonEdge.OrReaches x2 x7 (PEqualNP.TopForm.opnd x7 x15 ((1 : Fin (2 : ℕ)) - x16)) x17 ∧ PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.gateVal x2 x7 x17 (PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.pairInput x13 x14) = Bool.true}.card ≤ x1 * (x7.size + (1 : ℕ)) := by
  exact ⟨54, fun _ G _ hgirth _ C hLeast hTF _ hNoCone =>
    PEqualNP.Outside.L_meta_3805.main_bound G hgirth C hLeast hTF hNoCone⟩
