theorem PEqualNP.Outside.R_meta_2260_1
    (coverCost : ∀ (H : SimpleGraph (Fin (11 : ℕ))) [inst : DecidableRel H.Adj],
      let S : Finset (Fin (11 : ℕ)) := {(3 : Fin (11 : ℕ)), (8 : Fin (11 : ℕ)), (9 : Fin (11 : ℕ)), (10 : Fin (11 : ℕ))};
      have M : Finset (Fin (11 : ℕ) × Fin (11 : ℕ)) := {((1 : Fin (11 : ℕ)), (4 : Fin (11 : ℕ))), ((2 : Fin (11 : ℕ)), (5 : Fin (11 : ℕ))), ((6 : Fin (11 : ℕ)), (7 : Fin (11 : ℕ)))};
      (∀ (u v : Fin (11 : ℕ)), H.Adj u v → u ∈ S ∨ v ∈ S ∨ (u, v) ∈ M ∨ (v, u) ∈ M) →
      ∀ (K : Finset (Fin (11 : ℕ))),
      PEqualNP.MetaComplexity.NestedCover.IsMinCover H K →
      ∃ B ⊆ S,
      (∀ v ∈ S \ B, ∀ w ∈ S \ B, ¬H.Adj v w) ∧
      let forced : Finset (Fin (11 : ℕ)) := {v ∈ Finset.univ \ S | ∃ a ∈ S \ B, H.Adj a v};
      have pairs : Finset (Fin (11 : ℕ) × Fin (11 : ℕ)) := {e ∈ M | H.Adj e.1 e.2 ∧ e.1 ∉ forced ∧ e.2 ∉ forced};
      K.card = B.card + forced.card + pairs.card)
    (gateCharge : ∀ (G : SimpleGraph (Fin (11 : ℕ))) [inst : DecidableRel G.Adj] (C : PEqualNP.CircuitLowerBounds.Circuit),
      G = PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.edgeGraph (11 : ℕ) [((0 : ℕ), (9 : ℕ)), ((0 : ℕ), (10 : ℕ)), ((1 : ℕ), (4 : ℕ)), ((1 : ℕ), (8 : ℕ)), ((2 : ℕ), (5 : ℕ)), ((2 : ℕ), (10 : ℕ)), ((3 : ℕ), (6 : ℕ)), ((3 : ℕ), (7 : ℕ)), ((4 : ℕ), (8 : ℕ)), ((4 : ℕ), (10 : ℕ)), ((5 : ℕ), (8 : ℕ)), ((5 : ℕ), (9 : ℕ)), ((6 : ℕ), (7 : ℕ)), ((6 : ℕ), (10 : ℕ)), ((7 : ℕ), (9 : ℕ)), ((8 : ℕ), (9 : ℕ))] →
      PEqualNP.TopForm.IsLeastAO G C →
      PEqualNP.TopForm.TopForm G C →
      C.size < (22 : ℕ) →
      (∃ (i : ℕ) (j : ℕ), PEqualNP.TopForm.junk G C = {i, j} ∧ PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.InCone (11 : ℕ) C i j) →
      let U : Finset (Sym2 (Fin (11 : ℕ))) := PEqualNP.TopForm.Ustar G C;
      let H : SimpleGraph (Fin (11 : ℕ)) := G.deleteEdges ↑U;
      let S : Finset (Fin (11 : ℕ)) := {(3 : Fin (11 : ℕ)), (8 : Fin (11 : ℕ)), (9 : Fin (11 : ℕ)), (10 : Fin (11 : ℕ))};
      have M : Finset (Fin (11 : ℕ) × Fin (11 : ℕ)) := {((1 : Fin (11 : ℕ)), (4 : Fin (11 : ℕ))), ((2 : Fin (11 : ℕ)), (5 : Fin (11 : ℕ))), ((6 : Fin (11 : ℕ)), (7 : Fin (11 : ℕ)))};
      ∀ B ⊆ S,
      (∀ u ∈ S \ B, ∀ w ∈ S \ B, ¬H.Adj u w) →
      let forced : Finset (Fin (11 : ℕ)) := {v ∈ Finset.univ \ S | ∃ w ∈ S \ B, H.Adj w v};
      have pairs : Finset (Fin (11 : ℕ) × Fin (11 : ℕ)) := {e ∈ M | H.Adj e.1 e.2 ∧ e.1 ∉ forced ∧ e.2 ∉ forced};
      (5 : ℕ) + U.card ≤ B.card + forced.card + pairs.card + (2 : ℕ) * (PEqualNP.TopForm.centreless G C).card + (PEqualNP.TopForm.orN G C \ PEqualNP.TopForm.lam G C).card) :
    ∀ (x1 : SimpleGraph (Fin (11 : ℕ))) [x2 : DecidableRel x1.Adj] (x3 : PEqualNP.CircuitLowerBounds.Circuit) (x4 x5 : ℕ),   x1 = PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.edgeGraph (11 : ℕ) [((0 : ℕ), (9 : ℕ)), ((0 : ℕ), (10 : ℕ)), ((1 : ℕ), (4 : ℕ)), ((1 : ℕ), (8 : ℕ)), ((2 : ℕ), (5 : ℕ)), ((2 : ℕ), (10 : ℕ)), ((3 : ℕ), (6 : ℕ)), ((3 : ℕ), (7 : ℕ)), ((4 : ℕ), (8 : ℕ)), ((4 : ℕ), (10 : ℕ)), ((5 : ℕ), (8 : ℕ)), ((5 : ℕ), (9 : ℕ)), ((6 : ℕ), (7 : ℕ)), ((6 : ℕ), (10 : ℕ)), ((7 : ℕ), (9 : ℕ)), ((8 : ℕ), (9 : ℕ))] →     PEqualNP.TopForm.IsLeastAO x1 x3 →       PEqualNP.TopForm.TopForm x1 x3 →         have x9 : Finset ℕ := PEqualNP.TopForm.tops x1 x3;         have x10 : Finset ℕ := PEqualNP.TopForm.centreless x1 x3;         have x11 : Finset (Sym2 (Fin (11 : ℕ))) := PEqualNP.TopForm.Ustar x1 x3;         (∀ x12 ∈ x9,             have x14 : Fin (2 : ℕ) → Finset (Fin (11 : ℕ)) := PEqualNP.TopForm.side x3 x12;             (∀ x15 ∈ x14 (0 : Fin (2 : ℕ)), ∀ x17 ∈ x14 (1 : Fin (2 : ℕ)), x1.Adj x15 x17) ∧ ((x14 (0 : Fin (2 : ℕ))).card ≤ (1 : ℕ) ∨ (x14 (1 : Fin (2 : ℕ))).card ≤ (1 : ℕ))) →           (∀ (x13 x14 : Fin (11 : ℕ)), s(x13, x14) ∈ x11 → ∃ x16 ∈ x9, ∃ (x17 : Fin (2 : ℕ)) (x18 : ℕ), (x18 = x4 ∨ x18 = x5) ∧ PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.gateVal (11 : ℕ) x3 x16 (PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.pairInput x13 x14) = Bool.true ∧ PEqualNP.CleanedNonEdge.OrReaches (11 : ℕ) x3 (PEqualNP.TopForm.opnd x3 x16 x17) x18 ∧ PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.gateVal (11 : ℕ) x3 x18 (PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.pairInput x13 x14) = Bool.true) → (17 : ℕ) + (x9 \ x10).card + (2 : ℕ) * x10.card + (PEqualNP.TopForm.orN x1 x3 \ PEqualNP.TopForm.lam x1 x3).card ≤ x3.size + x11.card → (∀ (x15 : Finset (Fin (11 : ℕ))), PEqualNP.MetaComplexity.NestedCover.IsMinCover (x1.deleteEdges ↑x11) x15 → x15.card ≤ (x9 \ x10).card) → PEqualNP.TopForm.junk x1 x3 = {x4, x5} → PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.InCone (11 : ℕ) x3 x4 x5 → (22 : ℕ) ≤ x3.size := by
  intro G inst C i j hg hleast htop tops0 centre0 eqEdges hside hsource hcount hcovered hj hcone
  classical
  let U := Ustar G C
  let H := G.deleteEdges (U : Set _)
  let S := ({3, 8, 9, 10} : Finset (Fin 11))
  let M := ({(1, 4), (2, 5), (6, 7)} : Finset (Fin 11 × Fin 11))
  have graphShape : ∀ u v, (PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.edgeGraph 11 [(0,9),(0,10),(1,4),(1,8),(2,5),(2,10),(3,6),(3,7),(4,8),(4,10),(5,8),(5,9),(6,7),(6,10),(7,9),(8,9)]).Adj u v →
      u ∈ S ∨ v ∈ S ∨ (u,v) ∈ M ∨ (v,u) ∈ M := by decide +kernel
  have shape : ∀ u v, H.Adj u v → u ∈ S ∨ v ∈ S ∨ (u,v) ∈ M ∨ (v,u) ∈ M := by
    intro u v huv
    apply graphShape u v
    rw [← hg]
    exact (SimpleGraph.deleteEdges_adj.mp huv).1
  let P := fun k : ℕ => ∃ K : Finset (Fin 11), H.IsVertexCover (K : Set _) ∧ K.card = k
  have ex : ∃ k, P k := ⟨11, Finset.univ, by simp [SimpleGraph.IsVertexCover], by simp⟩
  obtain ⟨K, covers, hk⟩ := Nat.find_spec ex
  have hmin : IsMinCover H K := by
    refine ⟨covers, ?_⟩
    intro A ha
    rw [hk]
    exact Nat.find_min' ex (show P A.card from ⟨A, ha, rfl⟩)
  obtain ⟨B, hB, valid, cost⟩ := coverCost H shape K hmin
  by_contra short
  have small : C.size < 22 := by omega
  have charge := gateCharge G C hg hleast htop small ⟨i,j,hj,hcone⟩ B hB valid
  have bound := hcovered K hmin
  change 17 + (tops G C \ centreless G C).card + 2 * (centreless G C).card +
    (orN G C \ lam G C).card ≤ C.size + U.card at hcount
  let F := (Finset.univ \ S).filter (fun v => ∃ a ∈ S \ B, H.Adj a v)
  let L := M.filter (fun e => H.Adj e.1 e.2 ∧ e.1 ∉ F ∧ e.2 ∉ F)
  change K.card = B.card + F.card + L.card at cost
  change (5 + U.card) ≤ B.card + F.card + L.card + 2 * (centreless G C).card +
    (orN G C \ lam G C).card at charge
  change K.card ≤ (tops G C \ centreless G C).card at bound
  omega

lean/PEqualNP/Outside/R_meta_1597_421.lean

theorem PEqualNP.Outside.R_meta_1597_421
  (faceBound : let G := edgeGraph 11 [(0, 9), (0, 10), (1, 4), (1, 8), (2, 5), (2, 10), (3, 6), (3, 7), (4, 8), (4, 10), (5, 8), (5, 9), (6, 7), (6, 10), (7, 9), (8, 9)]
    ∀ (v : Fin 11) (E : Circuit), v.val < 4 → IsAO2 11 E →
      (∀ x : Fin 11 → Bool, x v = false → circuitFun 11 E x = graphFun G ∅ x) →
      19 ≤ E.size)
  (repeats : let G := edgeGraph 11 [(0, 9), (0, 10), (1, 4), (1, 8), (2, 5), (2, 10), (3, 6), (3, 7), (4, 8), (4, 10), (5, 8), (5, 9), (6, 7), (6, 10), (7, 9), (8, 9)]
    ∀ D : Circuit, IsLeastAO G D → TopForm G D → 2 ≤ (junk G D).card → D.size ≤ 21 →
      2 * (centreless G D).card + (junk G D).card + (orN G D \ lam G D).card <
        2 * (Ustar G D).card →
      ∃ v : Fin 11, v.val < 4 ∧
        let repeatsAt := fun j : ℕ => ∃ w < 11 + j,
          ∀ x : Fin 11 → Bool, x v = false → wval D x (11 + j) = wval D x w
        ∃ i j k : ℕ, i < j ∧ j < k ∧ k < D.size ∧ repeatsAt i ∧ repeatsAt j ∧ repeatsAt k) :
  (∀ (n : ℕ) (G : SimpleGraph (Fin n)) [DecidableRel G.Adj] (C : PEqualNP.CircuitLowerBounds.Circuit), PEqualNP.TopForm.NoFourCycle G → G.edgeFinset.Nonempty → PEqualNP.TopForm.IsLeastAO G C → PEqualNP.TopForm.TopForm G C → (∀ a ∈ PEqualNP.TopForm.tops G C, Disjoint (PEqualNP.TopForm.side (n := n) C a 0) (PEqualNP.TopForm.side (n := n) C a 1) ∧ (∀ u ∈ PEqualNP.TopForm.side (n := n) C a 0, ∀ v ∈ PEqualNP.TopForm.side (n := n) C a 1, G.Adj u v) ∧ ((PEqualNP.TopForm.side (n := n) C a 0).card ≤ 1 ∨ (PEqualNP.TopForm.side (n := n) C a 1).card ≤ 1)) ∧ ∀ u v : Fin n, s(u, v) ∈ PEqualNP.TopForm.Ustar G C → ∃ a ∈ PEqualNP.TopForm.tops G C, ∃ b : Fin 2, ∃ h ∈ PEqualNP.TopForm.junk G C, PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.gateVal n C a (PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.pairInput u v) = true ∧ PEqualNP.CleanedNonEdge.OrReaches n C (PEqualNP.TopForm.opnd C a b) h ∧ PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.gateVal n C h (PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.pairInput u v) = true) → (∀ (n : ℕ) (G : SimpleGraph (Fin n)) [DecidableRel G.Adj] (C : PEqualNP.CircuitLowerBounds.Circuit), PEqualNP.TopForm.NoFourCycle G → G.edgeFinset.Nonempty → PEqualNP.TopForm.IsLeastAO G C → PEqualNP.TopForm.TopForm G C → G.edgeFinset.card + (PEqualNP.TopForm.tops G C \ PEqualNP.TopForm.centreless G C).card + 2 * (PEqualNP.TopForm.centreless G C).card + (PEqualNP.TopForm.junk G C).card + (PEqualNP.TopForm.orN G C \ PEqualNP.TopForm.lam G C).card ≤ C.size + (PEqualNP.TopForm.Ustar G C).card + 1 ∧ ∀ K : Finset (Fin n), PEqualNP.MetaComplexity.NestedCover.IsMinCover (G.deleteEdges ↑(PEqualNP.TopForm.Ustar G C)) K → K.card ≤ (PEqualNP.TopForm.tops G C \ PEqualNP.TopForm.centreless G C).card) → ∀ D : PEqualNP.CircuitLowerBounds.Circuit, PEqualNP.TopForm.IsLeastAO (PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.edgeGraph 11 [(0, 9), (0, 10), (1, 4), (1, 8), (2, 5), (2, 10), (3, 6), (3, 7), (4, 8), (4, 10), (5, 8), (5, 9), (6, 7), (6, 10), (7, 9), (8, 9)]) D → PEqualNP.TopForm.TopForm (PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.edgeGraph 11 [(0, 9), (0, 10), (1, 4), (1, 8), (2, 5), (2, 10), (3, 6), (3, 7), (4, 8), (4, 10), (5, 8), (5, 9), (6, 7), (6, 10), (7, 9), (8, 9)]) D → 2 ≤ (PEqualNP.TopForm.junk (PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.edgeGraph 11 [(0, 9), (0, 10), (1, 4), (1, 8), (2, 5), (2, 10), (3, 6), (3, 7), (4, 8), (4, 10), (5, 8), (5, 9), (6, 7), (6, 10), (7, 9), (8, 9)]) D).card → 22 ≤ D.size := by
  classical
  intro routes pre D hm ht hj
  let stepWire (w : List Bool) (g : Gate) : List Bool :=
    w ++ [g.op.eval (g.inputs.map fun i => w.getD i false)]

  have wires_eq_foldl (C : Circuit) (x : List Bool) :
      C.wires x = C.gates.foldl stepWire x := rfl

  have foldl_length (gs : List Gate) (w : List Bool) :
      (gs.foldl stepWire w).length = w.length + gs.length := by
    induction gs generalizing w with
    | nil => simp
    | cons g gs ih =>
      rw [List.foldl_cons, ih]
      simp [stepWire]
      omega

  have foldl_getD_lt2 (gs : List Gate) (w : List Bool) (i : ℕ) (hi : i < w.length) :
      (gs.foldl stepWire w).getD i false = w.getD i false := by
    induction gs generalizing w with
    | nil => rfl
    | cons g gs ih =>
      simp only [List.foldl_cons]
      have hlt : i < (stepWire w g).length := by simp [stepWire]; omega
      rw [ih (stepWire w g) hlt]
      simp [stepWire, List.getD_eq_getElem?_getD, List.getElem?_append_left hi]

  have getD_ge (w : List Bool) (i : ℕ) (hi : w.length ≤ i) : w.getD i false = false := by
    simp [List.getD_eq_getElem?_getD, List.getElem?_eq_none hi]

  have foldl_getD_gate (gs : List Gate) (w : List Bool) (j : ℕ) (hj : j < gs.length) :
      (gs.foldl stepWire w).getD (w.length + j) false =
        (gs[j]).op.eval ((gs[j]).inputs.map fun i =>
          if i < w.length + j then (gs.foldl stepWire w).getD i false else false) := by
    induction gs generalizing w j with
    | nil => simp at hj
    | cons g gs ih =>
      rw [List.foldl_cons]
      cases j with
      | zero =>
        have h1 : w.length < (stepWire w g).length := by simp [stepWire]
        rw [Nat.add_zero, foldl_getD_lt2 gs (stepWire w g) w.length h1]
        simp only [List.getElem_cons_zero]
        have h2 : (stepWire w g).getD w.length false =
            g.op.eval (g.inputs.map fun i => w.getD i false) := by
          simp [stepWire, List.getD_eq_getElem?_getD]
        rw [h2]
        congr 1
        apply List.map_congr_left
        intro i _
        by_cases hi : i < w.length
        · rw [ite_eq_left hi, foldl_getD_lt2 gs (stepWire w g) i (by simp [stepWire]; omega)]
          simp [stepWire, List.getD_eq_getElem?_getD, List.getElem?_append_left hi]
        · rw [ite_eq_right hi]; exact getD_ge w i (by omega)
      | succ j =>
        have hj' : j < gs.length := by simpa using hj
        have key := ih (stepWire w g) j hj'
        have hl : (stepWire w g).length = w.length + 1 := by simp [stepWire]
        rw [hl] at key
        have e : w.length + (j + 1) = w.length + 1 + j := by omega
        simp only [List.getElem_cons_succ]
        rw [e]
        exact key

  let val (C : Circuit) (x : List Bool) (u : ℕ) : Bool := (C.wires x).getD u false

  have eval_eq_val (C : Circuit) (x : List Bool) : C.eval x = val C x C.output := rfl

  have gateAt_eq {C : Circuit} {j : ℕ} (hj : j < C.gates.length) : gateAt C j = C.gates[j] := by
    change C.gates.getD j ⟨GateOp.or, []⟩ = C.gates[j]
    simp [List.getD_eq_getElem?_getD, hj]

  have gateAt_mem {C : Circuit} {j : ℕ} (hj : j < C.gates.length) : gateAt C j ∈ C.gates := by
    rw [gateAt_eq hj]; exact List.getElem_mem hj

  have val_input {n : ℕ} {C : Circuit} {x : List Bool} (hx : x.length = n) {u : ℕ} (hu : u < n) :
      val C x u = x.getD u false := by
    unfold val; rw [wires_eq_foldl]; exact foldl_getD_lt2 _ _ _ (by omega)

  have val_ge {n : ℕ} {C : Circuit} {x : List Bool} (hx : x.length = n) {u : ℕ}
      (hu : n + C.gates.length ≤ u) : val C x u = false := by
    unfold val; apply getD_ge; rw [wires_eq_foldl, foldl_length]; omega

  have val_gate {n : ℕ} {C : Circuit} {x : List Bool} (hx : x.length = n) {j : ℕ}
      (hj : j < C.gates.length) :
      val C x (n + j) = (gateAt C j).op.eval ((gateAt C j).inputs.map fun i =>
        if i < n + j then val C x i else false) := by
    subst hx
    rw [gateAt_eq hj]
    exact foldl_getD_gate C.gates x j hj

  let OpMono (R : Bool → Bool → Prop) : Prop :=
    ∀ (op : GateOp), (op = GateOp.and ∨ op = GateOp.or) → ∀ (l : List ℕ) (f g : ℕ → Bool),
      (∀ i ∈ l, R (f i) (g i)) → R (op.eval (l.map f)) (op.eval (l.map g))

  have opMono_eq : OpMono Eq := by
    intro op _ l f g h
    rw [List.map_congr_left h]

  have val_prefix {n : ℕ} {C D : Circuit} {x : List Bool} (hx : x.length = n) {m : ℕ}
      (hmC : m ≤ C.gates.length) (hmD : m ≤ D.gates.length)
      (hg : ∀ k < m, gateAt D k = gateAt C k) : ∀ u, u < n + m → val D x u = val C x u := by
    intro u
    induction u using Nat.strong_induction_on with
    | _ u ih =>
      intro hu
      by_cases hun : u < n
      · rw [val_input hx hun, val_input hx hun]
      · obtain ⟨k, rfl⟩ : ∃ k, u = n + k := ⟨u - n, by omega⟩
        have hk : k < m := by omega
        rw [val_gate hx (by omega), val_gate hx (by omega), hg k hk]
        congr 1
        apply List.map_congr_left
        intro i _
        by_cases hi : i < n + k
        · rw [ite_eq_left hi, ite_eq_left hi]; exact ih i hi (by omega)
        · rw [ite_eq_right hi, ite_eq_right hi]

  let aliasWire (w i u : ℕ) : ℕ := if u = w then i else if w < u then u - 1 else u

  let aliasGate (w i : ℕ) (g : Gate) : Gate := ⟨g.op, g.inputs.map (aliasWire w i)⟩

  let bypass (n : ℕ) (C : Circuit) (r i : ℕ) : Circuit :=
    ⟨(List.range (C.gates.length - 1)).map (fun k =>
        if k < r then gateAt C k else aliasGate (n + r) i (gateAt C (k + 1))),
      aliasWire (n + r) i C.output⟩

  have bypass_length (n : ℕ) (C : Circuit) (r i : ℕ) :
      (bypass n C r i).gates.length = C.gates.length - 1 := by simp [bypass]

  have bypass_output (n : ℕ) (C : Circuit) (r i : ℕ) :
      (bypass n C r i).output = aliasWire (n + r) i C.output := rfl

  have gateAt_bypass {n : ℕ} {C : Circuit} {r i k : ℕ} (hk : k < C.gates.length - 1) :
      gateAt (bypass n C r i) k =
        if k < r then gateAt C k else aliasGate (n + r) i (gateAt C (k + 1)) := by
    have hk' : k < (bypass n C r i).gates.length := by rw [bypass_length]; exact hk
    rw [gateAt_eq hk']
    simp [bypass]

  have bypass_val_lt {n : ℕ} {C : Circuit} {x : List Bool} (hx : x.length = n) {r i : ℕ}
      (hr : r < C.gates.length) : ∀ u, u < n + r → val (bypass n C r i) x u = val C x u := by
    apply val_prefix hx (by omega) (by rw [bypass_length]; omega)
    intro k hk
    rw [gateAt_bypass (by omega), ite_eq_left hk]

  have aliasWire_self (w i : ℕ) : aliasWire w i w = i := by simp [aliasWire]

  have aliasWire_lt_self {w i u : ℕ} (hu : u < w) : aliasWire w i u = u := by
    unfold aliasWire; rw [ite_eq_right (by omega), ite_eq_right (by omega)]

  have aliasWire_gt_self {w i u : ℕ} (hu : w < u) : aliasWire w i u = u - 1 := by
    unfold aliasWire; rw [ite_eq_right (by omega), ite_eq_left hu]

  have bypass_rel {R : Bool → Bool → Prop} (hrefl : ∀ b, R b b) (hmono : OpMono R)
      {n : ℕ} {C : Circuit} {x : List Bool} (hx : x.length = n)
      (hAO : ∀ k < C.gates.length,
        (gateAt C k).op = GateOp.and ∨ (gateAt C k).op = GateOp.or)
      {r i : ℕ} (hr : r < C.gates.length) (hi : i < n + r ∨ n + C.gates.length ≤ i)
      (hR : (∃ k, r < k ∧ k < C.gates.length ∧ n + r ∈ (gateAt C k).inputs) →
        R (val C x (n + r)) (val C x i)) :
      ∀ u, u ≠ n + r → R (val C x u) (val (bypass n C r i) x (aliasWire (n + r) i u)) := by
    intro u
    induction u using Nat.strong_induction_on with
    | _ u ih =>
      intro hne
      by_cases hlt : u < n + r
      · rw [aliasWire_lt_self hlt, bypass_val_lt hx hr u hlt]; exact hrefl _
      · rw [aliasWire_gt_self (by omega)]
        by_cases hge : n + C.gates.length ≤ u
        · rw [val_ge hx hge, val_ge hx (by rw [bypass_length]; omega)]; exact hrefl _
        · obtain ⟨k, rfl⟩ : ∃ k, u = n + (k + 1) := ⟨u - n - 1, by omega⟩
          have hk1 : k + 1 < C.gates.length := by omega
          have hkr : ¬ k < r := by omega
          have e : n + (k + 1) - 1 = n + k := by omega
          rw [e, val_gate hx hk1, val_gate hx (by rw [bypass_length]; omega),
            gateAt_bypass (by omega), ite_eq_right hkr]
          simp only [aliasGate, List.map_map]
          refine hmono _ (hAO _ hk1) _ _ _ ?_
          intro i' hi'
          simp only [Function.comp_apply]
          by_cases h1 : i' = n + r
          · subst h1
            rw [aliasWire_self, ite_eq_left (by omega)]
            have hgi : (if i < n + k then val (bypass n C r i) x i else false) = val C x i := by
              rcases hi with hi | hi
              · rw [ite_eq_left (by omega), bypass_val_lt hx hr i hi]
              · rw [ite_eq_right (by omega), val_ge hx hi]
            rw [hgi]
            exact hR ⟨k + 1, by omega, hk1, hi'⟩
          · by_cases h2 : i' < n + r
            · rw [aliasWire_lt_self h2, ite_eq_left (by omega), ite_eq_left (by omega),
                bypass_val_lt hx hr i' h2]
              exact hrefl _
            · by_cases h3 : i' < n + (k + 1)
              · have key := ih i' h3 h1
                rw [aliasWire_gt_self (by omega)] at key
                rw [aliasWire_gt_self (by omega), ite_eq_left h3, ite_eq_left (by omega)]
                exact key
              · rw [aliasWire_gt_self (by omega), ite_eq_right h3, ite_eq_right (by omega)]
                exact hrefl _

  have bypass_eval_rel {R : Bool → Bool → Prop} (hrefl : ∀ b, R b b) (hmono : OpMono R)
      {n : ℕ} {C : Circuit} {x : List Bool} (hx : x.length = n)
      (hAO : ∀ k < C.gates.length,
        (gateAt C k).op = GateOp.and ∨ (gateAt C k).op = GateOp.or)
      {r i : ℕ} (hr : r < C.gates.length) (hi : i < n + r ∨ n + C.gates.length ≤ i)
      (hR : R (val C x (n + r)) (val C x i)) :
      R (C.eval x) ((bypass n C r i).eval x) := by
    rw [eval_eq_val, eval_eq_val, bypass_output]
    by_cases h : C.output = n + r
    · have hgi : val (bypass n C r i) x i = val C x i := by
        rcases hi with hi | hi
        · exact bypass_val_lt hx hr i hi
        · rw [val_ge hx hi, val_ge hx (by rw [bypass_length]; omega)]
      rw [h, aliasWire_self, hgi]; exact hR
    · exact bypass_rel hrefl hmono hx hAO hr hi (fun _ => hR) _ h

  have bypass_eval_eq {n : ℕ} {C : Circuit} {x : List Bool} (hx : x.length = n)
      (hAO : ∀ k < C.gates.length,
        (gateAt C k).op = GateOp.and ∨ (gateAt C k).op = GateOp.or)
      {r i : ℕ} (hr : r < C.gates.length) (hi : i < n + r ∨ n + C.gates.length ≤ i)
      (hR : val C x (n + r) = val C x i) :
      (bypass n C r i).eval x = C.eval x :=
    (bypass_eval_rel (R := Eq) (fun _ => rfl) opMono_eq hx hAO hr hi hR).symm

  have bypass_mem {n : ℕ} {C : Circuit} {r i : ℕ} :
      ∀ g ∈ (bypass n C r i).gates, ∃ g' ∈ C.gates, g.op = g'.op ∧
        g.inputs.length = g'.inputs.length := by
    intro g hg
    simp only [bypass, List.mem_map, List.mem_range] at hg
    obtain ⟨k, hk, rfl⟩ := hg
    split_ifs with h
    · exact ⟨gateAt C k, gateAt_mem (by omega), rfl, rfl⟩
    · exact ⟨gateAt C (k + 1), gateAt_mem (by omega), rfl, by simp [aliasGate]⟩

  have aliasWire_lt {w i u m : ℕ} (hi : i < w) (hwm : w ≤ m) (hu : u < m + 1) :
      aliasWire w i u < m := by
    unfold aliasWire; split_ifs <;> omega

  have bypass_readsBack {n : ℕ} {C : Circuit} {r i : ℕ} (hi : i < n + r)
      (hRB : ∀ k < C.gates.length, ∀ u ∈ (gateAt C k).inputs, u < n + k) :
      ∀ k < (bypass n C r i).gates.length,
        ∀ u ∈ (gateAt (bypass n C r i) k).inputs, u < n + k := by
    intro k hk u hu
    rw [bypass_length] at hk
    rw [gateAt_bypass hk] at hu
    split_ifs at hu with h
    · exact hRB k (by omega) u hu
    · simp only [aliasGate, List.mem_map] at hu
      obtain ⟨u', hu', rfl⟩ := hu
      have := hRB (k + 1) (by omega) u' hu'
      exact aliasWire_lt hi (by omega) (by omega)
  have drop : ∀ (n : ℕ) (C : Circuit) (v : Fin n) (r w : ℕ),
      IsAO2 n C → r < C.size → w < n + r →
      (∀ x : Fin n → Bool, x v = false → wval C x (n + r) = wval C x w) →
      ∃ E : Circuit, IsAO2 n E ∧ E.size + 1 = C.size ∧
        (∀ x : Fin n → Bool, x v = false → circuitFun n E x = circuitFun n C x) ∧
        ∀ (x : Fin n → Bool) (u : ℕ), u < n + r → wval E x u = wval C x u := by
    intro n C v r w model hr hw heq
    change r < C.gates.length at hr
    let E := bypass n C r w
    have ops : ∀ j < C.gates.length, (gateAt C j).op = GateOp.and ∨
        (gateAt C j).op = GateOp.or := fun j hj => model.1 _ (gateAt_mem hj)
    have modelE : IsAO2 n E := by
      refine ⟨?_, bypass_readsBack hw model.2.1, ?_⟩
      · intro g hg
        obtain ⟨g', hmem, hop, _⟩ := bypass_mem g hg
        rw [hop]
        exact model.1 g' hmem
      · intro g hg
        obtain ⟨g', hmem, _, hlen⟩ := bypass_mem g hg
        exact hlen.trans (model.2.2 g' hmem)
    refine ⟨E, modelE, ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
    · change (bypass n C r w).gates.length + 1 = C.gates.length
      rw [bypass_length]
      omega
    · intro x hx
      exact bypass_eval_eq List.length_ofFn ops hr (Or.inl hw) (heq x hx)
    · intro x u hu
      exact bypass_val_lt List.length_ofFn hr u hu
  let G := edgeGraph 11 [(0, 9), (0, 10), (1, 4), (1, 8), (2, 5), (2, 10), (3, 6), (3, 7), (4, 8), (4, 10), (5, 8), (5, 9), (6, 7), (6, 10), (7, 9), (8, 9)]
  let U := Ustar G D
  let B := 2 * (centreless G D).card + (junk G D).card + (orN G D \ lam G D).card
  by_contra! short
  have h4 : NoFourCycle G := by dsimp [G]; decide +kernel
  have h16 : G.edgeFinset.card = 16 := by dsimp [G]; decide +kernel
  have h7 : ∀ K : Finset (Fin 11), G.IsVertexCover (K : Set (Fin 11)) → 7 ≤ K.card := by
    intro K hK
    classical
    let w (i : Fin 11) : ℕ := if i ∈ K then 1 else 0
    have ew (u v : Fin 11) (he : G.Adj u v) : 1 ≤ w u + w v := by
      rcases hK he with hu | hv
      · change u ∈ K at hu
        simp [w, hu]
      · change v ∈ K at hv
        simp [w, hv]
    have e0 := ew 1 4 (by decide +kernel)
    have e1 := ew 4 8 (by decide +kernel)
    have e2 := ew 8 1 (by decide +kernel)
    have e3 := ew 3 6 (by decide +kernel)
    have e4 := ew 6 7 (by decide +kernel)
    have e5 := ew 7 3 (by decide +kernel)
    have e6 := ew 0 9 (by decide +kernel)
    have e7 := ew 9 5 (by decide +kernel)
    have e8 := ew 5 2 (by decide +kernel)
    have e9 := ew 2 10 (by decide +kernel)
    have e10 := ew 10 0 (by decide +kernel)
    have hw : ∑ i, w i = K.card := by
      simp [w]
    rw [Fin.sum_univ_succ] at hw
    simp [Fin.sum_univ_succ] at hw
    omega
  have he : G.edgeFinset.Nonempty := Finset.card_pos.mp (by omega)
  obtain ⟨hs, hk⟩ := pre 11 G D h4 he hm ht
  rw [h16] at hs
  change 16 + (tops G D \ centreless G D).card + 2 * (centreless G D).card +
    (junk G D).card + (orN G D \ lam G D).card ≤ D.size + U.card + 1 at hs
  have acc : 16 + (tops G D \ centreless G D).card + B ≤ D.size + U.card + 1 := by
    dsimp [B]
    omega
  by_cases loose : 2 * U.card ≤ B
  · let H := G.deleteEdges (↑U)
    let P (m : ℕ) := ∃ K : Finset (Fin 11), H.IsVertexCover (↑K) ∧ K.card = m
    have existsP : ∃ m, P m := ⟨_, Finset.univ, by simp [H], rfl⟩
    obtain ⟨K, hK, hcard⟩ := Nat.find_spec existsP
    have hmin : IsMinCover H K := by
      refine ⟨hK, fun T hT => ?_⟩
      exact hcard.trans_le (Nat.find_min' existsP ⟨T, hT, rfl⟩)
    have kt := hk K hmin
    let X := U.image (fun e => e.out.1)
    have hx : X.card ≤ U.card := Finset.card_image_le
    have cover : G.IsVertexCover (↑(K ∪ X)) := by
      intro u v huv
      by_cases eh : s(u,v) ∈ U
      · have hvx : s(u,v).out.1 ∈ X := Finset.mem_image_of_mem _ eh
        have hh := Sym2.out_fst_mem s(u,v)
        rw [Sym2.mem_iff] at hh
        rcases hh with h | h
        · exact Or.inl (Finset.mem_union_right _ (h ▸ hvx))
        · exact Or.inr (Finset.mem_union_right _ (h ▸ hvx))
      · have hh : H.Adj u v := SimpleGraph.deleteEdges_adj.mpr ⟨huv, eh⟩
        rcases hK hh with h | h
        · exact Or.inl (Finset.mem_union_left X h)
        · exact Or.inr (Finset.mem_union_left X h)
    have score := h7 _ cover
    have card := Finset.card_union_le K X
    omega
  · have small : B < 2 * U.card := Nat.lt_of_not_ge loose
    obtain ⟨v, hv, i, j, k, hij, hjk, hk, ⟨wi, hwi, ei⟩, ⟨wj, hwj, ej⟩, wk, hwk, ek⟩ :=
      repeats D hm ht hj (by omega) small
    obtain ⟨E, mE, lE, oE, pE⟩ := drop 11 D v k wk hm.1 hk hwk ek
    have ejE (x : Fin 11 → Bool) (hx : x v = false) :
        wval E x (11 + j) = wval E x wj := by
      rw [pE x _ (by omega), pE x _ (by omega)]
      exact ej x hx
    obtain ⟨F, mF, lF, oF, pF⟩ := drop 11 E v j wj mE (by omega) hwj ejE
    have eiF (x : Fin 11 → Bool) (hx : x v = false) :
        wval F x (11 + i) = wval F x wi := by
      rw [pF x _ (by omega), pF x _ (by omega), pE x _ (by omega), pE x _ (by omega)]
      exact ei x hx
    obtain ⟨H, mH, lH, oH, _⟩ := drop 11 F v i wi mF (by omega) hwi eiF
    have face := faceBound v H hv mH (fun x hx =>
      (oH x hx).trans ((oF x hx).trans ((oE x hx).trans (congrFun hm.2.1 x))))
    omega


lean/PEqualNP/Outside/L_meta_1492.lean

theorem PEqualNP.Outside.L_meta_1492 : ∀ (n : ℕ) (G : SimpleGraph (Fin n)) [DecidableRel G.Adj] (C : PEqualNP.CircuitLowerBounds.Circuit),
  PEqualNP.TopForm.NoFourCycle G → G.edgeFinset.Nonempty → PEqualNP.TopForm.IsLeastAO G C →
  PEqualNP.TopForm.TopForm G C → ∀ (g : ℕ), PEqualNP.TopForm.junk G C = {g} →
    ∃ K : Finset (Fin n), (∀ e ∈ PEqualNP.TopForm.starEdges G C, ∃ v ∈ K, v ∈ e) ∧
      (Finset.univ : Finset (Fin n)).filter (fun u => ∃ a ∈ PEqualNP.TopForm.tops G C, ∃ b : Fin 2,
          PEqualNP.TopForm.side (n := n) C a b = {u} ∧
          PEqualNP.CleanedNonEdge.OrReaches n C (PEqualNP.TopForm.opnd C a b) g) ⊆ K ∧
      (Finset.univ : Finset (Fin n)).filter (fun w => ∃ a ∈ PEqualNP.TopForm.tops G C, ∃ b : Fin 2,
          2 ≤ (PEqualNP.TopForm.side (n := n) C a b).card ∧
          PEqualNP.CleanedNonEdge.OrReaches n C (PEqualNP.TopForm.opnd C a b) g ∧
          PEqualNP.TopForm.side (n := n) C a (1 - b) = {w}) ⊆ K ∧
      K.card + ((Finset.univ : Finset (Fin n)).filter (fun u => (∃ a ∈ PEqualNP.TopForm.tops G C, ∃ b : Fin 2,
          PEqualNP.TopForm.side (n := n) C a b = {u} ∧
          PEqualNP.CleanedNonEdge.OrReaches n C (PEqualNP.TopForm.opnd C a b) g) ∧
          ∃ v, G.Adj u v ∧ s(u, v) ∉ PEqualNP.TopForm.Ustar G C ∧ v ∉ PEqualNP.TopForm.wmax G C g ∧ v ∉ K)).card ≤
        (PEqualNP.TopForm.tops G C \ PEqualNP.TopForm.centreless G C).card := by
  exact PEqualNP.Outside.L_meta_1492.final


lean/PEqualNP/Outside/L_meta_1876.lean

theorem PEqualNP.Outside.L_meta_1876 : ∀ (x1 : ℕ) (x2 : SimpleGraph (Fin x1)) [x3 : DecidableRel x2.Adj], (∀ (x4 : Fin x1) (x5 : x2.Walk x4 x4), x5.IsCycle → (5 : ℕ) ≤ x5.length) → x2.edgeFinset.Nonempty → ∀ (x6 : PEqualNP.CircuitLowerBounds.Circuit), PEqualNP.TopForm.IsLeastAO x2 x6 → PEqualNP.TopForm.TopForm x2 x6 → PEqualNP.TopForm.junk x2 x6 = ∅ → PEqualNP.TopForm.Ustar x2 x6 = ∅ := by
  intro n G _ _ _ C hL hT hj
  by_contra hne
  obtain ⟨e, he⟩ := Finset.nonempty_iff_ne_empty.mpr hne
  revert he
  induction e using Sym2.ind with
  | _ u v =>
    intro huv
    rcases PEqualNP.Outside.L_meta_1876.off_star G C hL hT u v huv with
      ⟨_, _, _, _, _, _, x, hx, _⟩ | ⟨_, _, _, _, _, x, hx, _⟩
    · rw [hj] at hx; simp at hx
    · rw [hj] at hx; simp at hx


lean/PEqualNP/Outside/R_meta_2258_1.lean

theorem PEqualNP.Outside.R_meta_2258_1
    (hGraph :
      let G := edgeGraph 11 [(0, 9), (0, 10), (1, 4), (1, 8), (2, 5), (2, 10), (3, 6),
        (3, 7), (4, 8), (4, 10), (5, 8), (5, 9), (6, 7), (6, 10), (7, 9), (8, 9)]
      ∀ (U : Finset (Sym2 (Fin 11))) (K : Finset (Fin 11)),
        (G.deleteEdges ↑U).IsVertexCover (↑K : Set (Fin 11)) → 7 ≤ K.card + U.card)
    (hTrace :
      ∀ (n : ℕ) (G : SimpleGraph (Fin n)) [DecidableRel G.Adj] (D : Circuit),
        (∀ g ∈ D.gates, g.inputs.length = 2) →
        ∀ a ∈ tops G D, ∀ b : Fin 2, ∀ h ∈ junk G D,
          OrReaches n D (opnd D a b) h →
            a ∈ centreless G D ∨ (orN G D \ lam G D).Nonempty)
    (hCharge :
      ∀ (G : SimpleGraph (Fin 11)) [DecidableRel G.Adj] (D : Circuit),
        G = edgeGraph 11 [(0, 9), (0, 10), (1, 4), (1, 8), (2, 5), (2, 10), (3, 6),
          (3, 7), (4, 8), (4, 10), (5, 8), (5, 9), (6, 7), (6, 10), (7, 9), (8, 9)] →
        IsLeastAO G D → TopForm G D →
        let U := Ustar G D
        let J := junk G D
        let V := centreless G D
        let T := tops G D
        let R := orN G D \ lam G D
        (∀ a ∈ T, let S : Fin 2 → Finset (Fin 11) := side D a
          (∀ u ∈ S 0, ∀ v ∈ S 1, G.Adj u v) ∧ ((S 0).card ≤ 1 ∨ (S 1).card ≤ 1)) →
        (∀ u v : Fin 11, s(u, v) ∈ U → ∃ a ∈ T, ∃ b : Fin 2, ∃ h ∈ J,
          gateVal 11 D a (pairInput u v) = true ∧ OrReaches 11 D (opnd D a b) h ∧
          gateVal 11 D h (pairInput u v) = true) →
        3 ≤ U.card → 3 ≤ J.card → D.size ≤ 21 →
        ∃ K A : Finset (Fin 11),
          IsMinCover (G.deleteEdges ↑U) K ∧
          (∀ u v : Fin 11, s(u, v) ∈ U → u ∈ K ∪ A ∨ v ∈ K ∪ A) ∧
          A.card + U.card ≤ 2 * V.card + J.card + R.card)
    : ∀ (x1 : SimpleGraph (Fin (11 : ℕ))) [x2 : DecidableRel x1.Adj] (x3 : PEqualNP.CircuitLowerBounds.Circuit),   x1 = PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.edgeGraph (11 : ℕ) [((0 : ℕ), (9 : ℕ)), ((0 : ℕ), (10 : ℕ)), ((1 : ℕ), (4 : ℕ)), ((1 : ℕ), (8 : ℕ)), ((2 : ℕ), (5 : ℕ)), ((2 : ℕ), (10 : ℕ)), ((3 : ℕ), (6 : ℕ)), ((3 : ℕ), (7 : ℕ)), ((4 : ℕ), (8 : ℕ)), ((4 : ℕ), (10 : ℕ)), ((5 : ℕ), (8 : ℕ)), ((5 : ℕ), (9 : ℕ)), ((6 : ℕ), (7 : ℕ)), ((6 : ℕ), (10 : ℕ)), ((7 : ℕ), (9 : ℕ)), ((8 : ℕ), (9 : ℕ))] →     PEqualNP.TopForm.IsLeastAO x1 x3 →       PEqualNP.TopForm.TopForm x1 x3 →         have x7 : Finset ℕ := PEqualNP.TopForm.tops x1 x3;         have x8 : Finset ℕ := PEqualNP.TopForm.centreless x1 x3;         have x9 : Finset ℕ := PEqualNP.TopForm.junk x1 x3;         have x10 : Finset (Sym2 (Fin (11 : ℕ))) := PEqualNP.TopForm.Ustar x1 x3;         (∀ x11 ∈ x7,             have x13 : Fin (2 : ℕ) → Finset (Fin (11 : ℕ)) := PEqualNP.TopForm.side x3 x11;             (∀ x14 ∈ x13 (0 : Fin (2 : ℕ)), ∀ x16 ∈ x13 (1 : Fin (2 : ℕ)), x1.Adj x14 x16) ∧ ((x13 (0 : Fin (2 : ℕ))).card ≤ (1 : ℕ) ∨ (x13 (1 : Fin (2 : ℕ))).card ≤ (1 : ℕ))) →           (∀ (x12 x13 : Fin (11 : ℕ)), s(x12, x13) ∈ x10 → ∃ x15 ∈ x7, ∃ (x16 : Fin (2 : ℕ)), ∃ x17 ∈ x9, PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.gateVal (11 : ℕ) x3 x15 (PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.pairInput x12 x13) = Bool.true ∧ PEqualNP.CleanedNonEdge.OrReaches (11 : ℕ) x3 (PEqualNP.TopForm.opnd x3 x15 x16) x17 ∧ PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.gateVal (11 : ℕ) x3 x17 (PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.pairInput x12 x13) = Bool.true) → (15 : ℕ) + (x7 \ x8).card + (2 : ℕ) * x8.card + x9.card + (PEqualNP.TopForm.orN x1 x3 \ PEqualNP.TopForm.lam x1 x3).card ≤ x3.size + x10.card → (∀ (x14 : Finset (Fin (11 : ℕ))), PEqualNP.MetaComplexity.NestedCover.IsMinCover (x1.deleteEdges ↑x10) x14 → x14.card ≤ (x7 \ x8).card) → (3 : ℕ) ≤ x9.card → (22 : ℕ) ≤ x3.size := by
  intro G _ D hG hLeast hTop
  dsimp only
  intro hStars hOff hCount hCoverBound hJunk
  by_contra hShort
  have hSize : D.size ≤ 21 := by omega
  let U := Ustar G D
  let V := centreless G D
  let T := tops G D
  let J := junk G D
  let R := orN G D \ lam G D
  change 15 + (T \ V).card + 2 * V.card + J.card + R.card ≤ D.size + U.card at hCount
  change 3 ≤ J.card at hJunk
  have hResidual (W : Finset (Sym2 (Fin 11))) (K : Finset (Fin 11))
      (hK : (G.deleteEdges ↑W).IsVertexCover (↑K : Set (Fin 11))) : 7 ≤ K.card + W.card := by
    rw [hG] at hK
    exact hGraph W K hK
  classical
  let H := G.deleteEdges (↑U : Set (Sym2 (Fin 11)))
  by_cases hLarge : 3 ≤ U.card
  · obtain ⟨K, A, hKMin, hA, hACharge⟩ := hCharge G D hG hLeast hTop hStars hOff hLarge hJunk hSize
    have hKT : K.card ≤ (T \ V).card := hCoverBound K hKMin
    change A.card + U.card ≤ 2 * V.card + J.card + R.card at hACharge
    have hFull : G.IsVertexCover (↑(K ∪ A) : Set (Fin 11)) := by
      intro u v hadj
      by_cases hDel : s(u, v) ∈ U
      · exact hA u v hDel
      · have he : H.Adj u v := SimpleGraph.deleteEdges_adj.mpr ⟨hadj, hDel⟩
        exact (hKMin.1 he).imp (Finset.mem_union_left _) (Finset.mem_union_left _)
    have hFullDeleted : (G.deleteEdges (↑(∅ : Finset (Sym2 (Fin 11))) : Set (Sym2 (Fin 11)))).IsVertexCover
        (↑(K ∪ A) : Set (Fin 11)) := by
      intro u v hadj
      exact hFull (SimpleGraph.deleteEdges_adj.mp hadj).1
    have hFullBound := hResidual ∅ (K ∪ A) hFullDeleted
    have hUnion := Finset.card_union_le K A
    rw [Finset.card_empty, Nat.add_zero] at hFullBound
    omega
  · let covers : Finset (Finset (Fin 11)) :=
      Finset.univ.filter (fun (K : Finset (Fin 11)) => H.IsVertexCover (↑K : Set (Fin 11)))
    have hCovers : covers.Nonempty := by
      refine ⟨(Finset.univ : Finset (Fin 11)), Finset.mem_filter.mpr ⟨Finset.mem_univ _, ?_⟩⟩
      intro u v _
      exact Or.inl (Finset.mem_univ u)
    obtain ⟨K, hK, hMin⟩ := Finset.exists_min_image covers Finset.card hCovers
    have hKMin : IsMinCover H K :=
      ⟨(Finset.mem_filter.mp hK).2, fun A hA =>
        hMin A (Finset.mem_filter.mpr ⟨Finset.mem_univ _, hA⟩)⟩
    have hKT : K.card ≤ (T \ V).card := hCoverBound K hKMin
    have hKBound := hResidual U K hKMin.1
    have hUNonempty : U.Nonempty := Finset.card_pos.mp (by omega)
    obtain ⟨e, he⟩ := hUNonempty
    induction e using Sym2.ind with
    | _ u v =>
      obtain ⟨a, ha, b, h, hh, _, hr, _⟩ := hOff u v he
      rcases hTrace 11 G D hLeast.1.2.2 a ha b h hh hr with haV | hR
      · have hV : 0 < V.card := Finset.card_pos.mpr ⟨a, haV⟩
        omega
      · have hR' : 0 < R.card := Finset.card_pos.mpr hR
        omega


lean/PEqualNP/Outside/R_meta_1770_1.lean

theorem PEqualNP.Outside.R_meta_1770_1
    (bothSing : ∃ k : ℕ, ∀ (n : ℕ) (G : SimpleGraph (Fin n)) [DecidableRel G.Adj] (C : Circuit),
      (∀ (v : Fin n) (p : G.Walk v v), p.IsCycle → 5 ≤ p.length) →
      IsLeastAO G C → PEqualNP.TopForm.TopForm G C →
      ((Ustar G C).filter (fun e => ∃ u v : Fin n, e = s(u, v) ∧
        ∃ a ∈ tops G C,
          wireVal n C (opnd C a 0) (pairInput u u) = true ∧
          wireVal n C (opnd C a 1) (pairInput v v) = true)).card ≤ k * (C.size + 1))
    (oneSing : ∃ k : ℕ, ∀ (n : ℕ) (G : SimpleGraph (Fin n)) [DecidableRel G.Adj] (C : Circuit),
      (∀ (v : Fin n) (p : G.Walk v v), p.IsCycle → 5 ≤ p.length) →
      IsLeastAO G C → PEqualNP.TopForm.TopForm G C →
      ((Ustar G C).filter (fun e => ∃ u v : Fin n, e = s(u, v) ∧
        ∃ g ∈ junk G C, u ∈ wmax G C g ∧
          gateVal n C g (pairInput u v) = true ∧
          gateVal n C g (pairInput u u) = false ∧
          gateVal n C g (pairInput v v) = false)).card ≤ k * (C.size + 1))
    (bothQuad : ∃ k : ℕ, ∀ (n : ℕ) (G : SimpleGraph (Fin n)) [DecidableRel G.Adj] (C : Circuit),
      (∀ (v : Fin n) (p : G.Walk v v), p.IsCycle → 5 ≤ p.length) →
      IsLeastAO G C → PEqualNP.TopForm.TopForm G C →
      ((Ustar G C).filter (fun e => ∃ u v : Fin n, e = s(u, v) ∧
        ∃ a ∈ tops G C, ∃ g ∈ junk G C, ∃ h ∈ junk G C, g ≠ h ∧
          OrReaches n C (opnd C a 0) g ∧ OrReaches n C (opnd C a 1) h ∧
          gateVal n C g (pairInput u v) = true ∧ gateVal n C h (pairInput u v) = true ∧
          gateVal n C g (pairInput u u) = false ∧ gateVal n C g (pairInput v v) = false ∧
          gateVal n C h (pairInput u u) = false ∧ gateVal n C h (pairInput v v) = false)).card ≤ k * (C.size + 1)) :
    ∃ (x1 : ℕ), ∀ (x2 : ℕ) (x3 : SimpleGraph (Fin x2)) [x4 : DecidableRel x3.Adj] (x5 : PEqualNP.CircuitLowerBounds.Circuit), (∀ (x6 : Fin x2) (x7 : x3.Walk x6 x6), x7.IsCycle → (5 : ℕ) ≤ x7.length) → PEqualNP.TopForm.IsLeastAO x3 x5 → PEqualNP.TopForm.TopForm x3 x5 → {x9 ∈ PEqualNP.TopForm.Ustar x3 x5 | (¬∃ (x10 : Fin x2) (x11 : Fin x2), x9 = s(x10, x11) ∧ ∃ x12 ∈ PEqualNP.TopForm.tops x3 x5, ∃ (x13 : Fin (2 : ℕ)), ∃ x14 ∈ PEqualNP.TopForm.junk x3 x5, x10 ∈ PEqualNP.TopForm.side x5 x12 x13 ∧ PEqualNP.CleanedNonEdge.OrReaches x2 x5 (PEqualNP.TopForm.opnd x5 x12 ((1 : Fin (2 : ℕ)) - x13)) x14 ∧ PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.gateVal x2 x5 x14 (PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.pairInput x10 x11) = Bool.true) ∧ ∃ (x10 : Fin x2) (x11 : Fin x2), x9 = s(x10, x11) ∧ ∃ x12 ∈ PEqualNP.TopForm.tops x3 x5, ∃ x13 ∈ PEqualNP.TopForm.junk x3 x5, ∃ x14 ∈ PEqualNP.TopForm.junk x3 x5, x13 ≠ x14 ∧ PEqualNP.CleanedNonEdge.OrReaches x2 x5 (PEqualNP.TopForm.opnd x5 x12 (0 : Fin (2 : ℕ))) x13 ∧ PEqualNP.CleanedNonEdge.OrReaches x2 x5 (PEqualNP.TopForm.opnd x5 x12 (1 : Fin (2 : ℕ))) x14 ∧ PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.gateVal x2 x5 x13 (PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.pairInput x10 x11) = Bool.true ∧ PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.gateVal x2 x5 x14 (PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.pairInput x10 x11) = Bool.true}.card ≤ x1 * (x5.size + (1 : ℕ)) := by
  obtain ⟨k1, hk1⟩ := bothSing
  obtain ⟨k2, hk2⟩ := oneSing
  obtain ⟨k3, hk3⟩ := bothQuad
  refine ⟨k1 + k2 + k3, ?_⟩
  intro n G _ C hg hL hT
  letI := PEqualNP.MetaComplexity.NestedCover.gateOpDecEq
  have h1 := hk1 n G C hg hL hT
  have h2 := hk2 n G C hg hL hT
  have h3 := hk3 n G C hg hL hT
  obtain ⟨⟨hAO, hRB, hlen2⟩, hfun, _⟩ := hL
  let step : List Bool → Gate → List Bool := fun w g =>
    w ++ [g.op.eval (g.inputs.map fun i => w.getD i false)]
  have step_len : ∀ (w : List Bool) (g : Gate), (step w g).length = w.length + 1 := by
    intro w g
    change (w ++ [g.op.eval (g.inputs.map fun i => w.getD i false)]).length = w.length + 1
    rw [List.length_append, List.length_singleton]
  have step_getD_lt : ∀ (w : List Bool) (g : Gate) (i : ℕ), i < w.length →
      (step w g).getD i false = w.getD i false := by
    intro w g i hi
    change (w ++ [g.op.eval (g.inputs.map fun i => w.getD i false)]).getD i false = w.getD i false
    rw [List.getD_eq_getElem?_getD, List.getElem?_append_left hi, ← List.getD_eq_getElem?_getD]
  have step_getD_self : ∀ (w : List Bool) (g : Gate),
      (step w g).getD w.length false = g.op.eval (g.inputs.map fun i => w.getD i false) := by
    intro w g
    change (w ++ [g.op.eval (g.inputs.map fun i => w.getD i false)]).getD w.length false = _
    rw [List.getD_eq_getElem?_getD, List.getElem?_append_right le_rfl, Nat.sub_self]
    rfl
  have getD_ge : ∀ (w : List Bool) (i : ℕ), w.length ≤ i → w.getD i false = false := by
    intro w i hi
    rw [List.getD_eq_getElem?_getD, List.getElem?_eq_none hi]
    rfl
  have foldl_len : ∀ (gs : List Gate) (w : List Bool),
      (gs.foldl step w).length = w.length + gs.length := by
    intro gs
    induction gs with
    | nil =>
      intro w
      rw [List.foldl_nil, List.length_nil, Nat.add_zero]
    | cons g gs ih =>
      intro w
      rw [List.foldl_cons, ih, step_len, List.length_cons]
      omega
  have foldl_getD_lt : ∀ (gs : List Gate) (w : List Bool) (i : ℕ), i < w.length →
      (gs.foldl step w).getD i false = w.getD i false := by
    intro gs
    induction gs with
    | nil => intro w i _; rfl
    | cons g gs ih =>
      intro w i hi
      rw [List.foldl_cons, ih (step w g) i (by rw [step_len]; omega)]
      exact step_getD_lt w g i hi
  have foldl_getD_gate : ∀ (gs : List Gate) (w : List Bool) (j : ℕ) (hj : j < gs.length),
      (gs.foldl step w).getD (w.length + j) false =
        (gs[j]).op.eval ((gs[j]).inputs.map fun i =>
          if i < w.length + j then (gs.foldl step w).getD i false else false) := by
    intro gs
    induction gs with
    | nil =>
      intro w j hj
      rw [List.length_nil] at hj
      exact (Nat.not_lt_zero j hj).elim
    | cons g gs ih =>
      intro w j hj
      rw [List.foldl_cons]
      cases j with
      | zero =>
        rw [Nat.add_zero, foldl_getD_lt gs (step w g) w.length (by rw [step_len]; omega),
          step_getD_self]
        rw [List.getElem_cons_zero]
        congr 1
        apply List.map_congr_left
        intro i _
        by_cases hi : i < w.length
        · rw [ite_eq_left_of_eq_true _ _ (eq_true hi),
            foldl_getD_lt gs (step w g) i (by rw [step_len]; omega), step_getD_lt w g i hi]
        · rw [ite_eq_right_of_eq_false _ _ (eq_false hi), getD_ge w i (Nat.le_of_not_lt hi)]
      | succ j =>
        have hj' : j < gs.length := Nat.lt_of_succ_lt_succ hj
        have key := ih (step w g) j hj'
        rw [step_len] at key
        have e : w.length + (j + 1) = w.length + 1 + j := by omega
        rw [List.getElem_cons_succ]
        change (gs.foldl step (step w g)).getD (w.length + (j + 1)) false = _
        rw [e]
        exact key
  have foldl_mono : ∀ (gs : List Gate) (w1 w2 : List Bool),
      (∀ g ∈ gs, g.op = GateOp.and ∨ g.op = GateOp.or) → w1.length = w2.length →
      (∀ i, w1.getD i false = true → w2.getD i false = true) →
      ∀ i, (gs.foldl step w1).getD i false = true → (gs.foldl step w2).getD i false = true := by
    intro gs
    induction gs with
    | nil => intro w1 w2 _ _ h i hi; exact h i hi
    | cons g gs ih =>
      intro w1 w2 hgs hlen hw i hi
      rw [List.foldl_cons] at hi ⊢
      refine ih (step w1 g) (step w2 g) (fun x hx => hgs x (List.mem_cons_of_mem g hx))
        (by rw [step_len, step_len, hlen]) ?_ i hi
      intro k hk
      rcases Nat.lt_trichotomy k w1.length with hklt | rfl | hkgt
      · rw [step_getD_lt w1 g k hklt] at hk
        rw [step_getD_lt w2 g k (hlen ▸ hklt)]
        exact hw k hk
      · rw [step_getD_self] at hk
        rw [hlen, step_getD_self]
        rcases hgs g List.mem_cons_self with hop | hop
        · rw [hop] at hk ⊢
          change (g.inputs.map fun t => w1.getD t false).all id = true at hk
          change (g.inputs.map fun t => w2.getD t false).all id = true
          rw [List.all_map, List.all_eq_true] at hk ⊢
          intro t ht
          exact hw t (hk t ht)
        · rw [hop] at hk ⊢
          change (g.inputs.map fun t => w1.getD t false).any id = true at hk
          change (g.inputs.map fun t => w2.getD t false).any id = true
          rw [List.any_map, List.any_eq_true] at hk ⊢
          obtain ⟨t, ht, htv⟩ := hk
          exact ⟨t, ht, hw t htv⟩
      · rw [getD_ge (step w1 g) k (by rw [step_len]; omega)] at hk
        cases hk
  have gateVal_mono : ∀ (j : ℕ) (x y : Fin n → Bool),
      (∀ i : Fin n, x i = true → y i = true) →
      gateVal n C j x = true → gateVal n C j y = true := by
    intro j x y hxy hj
    refine foldl_mono C.gates (List.ofFn x) (List.ofFn y) hAO
      (by rw [List.length_ofFn, List.length_ofFn]) ?_ (n + j) hj
    intro i hi
    by_cases hin : i < n
    · have hi1 : (List.ofFn x).getD i false = x ⟨i, hin⟩ := by
        rw [List.getD_eq_getElem?_getD,
          List.getElem?_eq_getElem (by rw [List.length_ofFn]; exact hin), List.getElem_ofFn]
        rfl
      have hi2 : (List.ofFn y).getD i false = y ⟨i, hin⟩ := by
        rw [List.getD_eq_getElem?_getD,
          List.getElem?_eq_getElem (by rw [List.length_ofFn]; exact hin), List.getElem_ofFn]
        rfl
      rw [hi1] at hi
      rw [hi2]
      exact hxy ⟨i, hin⟩ hi
    · rw [getD_ge (List.ofFn x) i (by rw [List.length_ofFn]; omega)] at hi
      cases hi
  have orStep_len : ∀ (cs : List (List ℕ)) (gt : Gate),
      (orStep n cs gt).length = cs.length + 1 := by
    intro cs gt
    change (cs ++ [cs.length :: (if gt.op = GateOp.or then
      (gt.inputs.filter (fun w => decide (n ≤ w ∧ w < n + cs.length))).flatMap
        (fun w => cs.getD (w - n) [])
      else [])]).length = cs.length + 1
    rw [List.length_append, List.length_singleton]
  have orReaches_inv : ∀ (gs : List Gate) (w : List Bool) (cs : List (List ℕ)),
      w.length = n + cs.length →
      (∀ k < cs.length, ∀ g ∈ cs.getD k [], g < cs.length ∧
        (w.getD (n + g) false = true → w.getD (n + k) false = true)) →
      (gs.foldl step w).length = n + (gs.foldl (orStep n) cs).length ∧
      ∀ k < (gs.foldl (orStep n) cs).length,
        ∀ g ∈ (gs.foldl (orStep n) cs).getD k [],
          g < (gs.foldl (orStep n) cs).length ∧
            ((gs.foldl step w).getD (n + g) false = true →
              (gs.foldl step w).getD (n + k) false = true) := by
    intro gs
    induction gs with
    | nil => intro w cs hlen hinv; exact ⟨hlen, hinv⟩
    | cons gt gs ih =>
      intro w cs hlen hinv
      rw [List.foldl_cons, List.foldl_cons]
      refine ih (step w gt) (orStep n cs gt)
        (by rw [step_len, orStep_len, hlen]; omega) ?_
      intro k hk g hg
      rw [orStep_len] at hk
      rcases Nat.lt_or_eq_of_le (Nat.lt_succ_iff.mp hk) with hklt | hkeq
      · have hcs_eq : (orStep n cs gt).getD k [] = cs.getD k [] := by
          change (cs ++ [_]).getD k [] = cs.getD k []
          rw [List.getD_eq_getElem?_getD, List.getElem?_append_left hklt,
            ← List.getD_eq_getElem?_getD]
        rw [hcs_eq] at hg
        obtain ⟨hg_lt, hval⟩ := hinv k hklt g hg
        refine ⟨by rw [orStep_len]; omega, fun hgv => ?_⟩
        rw [step_getD_lt w gt (n + g) (by omega)] at hgv
        rw [step_getD_lt w gt (n + k) (by omega)]
        exact hval hgv
      · subst hkeq
        have hcs_self : (orStep n cs gt).getD cs.length [] =
            cs.length :: (if gt.op = GateOp.or then
              (gt.inputs.filter (fun i => decide (n ≤ i ∧ i < n + cs.length))).flatMap
                (fun i => cs.getD (i - n) [])
              else []) := by
          change (cs ++ [_]).getD cs.length [] = _
          rw [List.getD_eq_getElem?_getD, List.getElem?_append_right le_rfl, Nat.sub_self]
          rfl
        rw [hcs_self, List.mem_cons] at hg
        rcases hg with rfl | hg
        · exact ⟨by rw [orStep_len]; omega, fun h => h⟩
        · split_ifs at hg with hop
          · rw [List.mem_flatMap] at hg
            obtain ⟨i, hi_filt, hg_mem⟩ := hg
            rw [List.mem_filter, decide_eq_true_eq] at hi_filt
            obtain ⟨hi_in, hni, hilt⟩ := hi_filt
            obtain ⟨hg_lt, hval⟩ := hinv (i - n) (by omega) g hg_mem
            refine ⟨by rw [orStep_len]; omega, fun hgv => ?_⟩
            rw [step_getD_lt w gt (n + g) (by omega)] at hgv
            have hwi : w.getD i false = true := by
              have heq : n + (i - n) = i := by omega
              rw [← heq]
              exact hval hgv
            rw [← hlen, step_getD_self, hop]
            change (gt.inputs.map fun k => w.getD k false).any id = true
            rw [List.any_map, List.any_eq_true]
            exact ⟨i, hi_in, hwi⟩
          · exact (List.not_mem_nil hg).elim
  have orReaches_up : ∀ (w g : ℕ) (x : Fin n → Bool),
      OrReaches n C w g → gateVal n C g x = true → wireVal n C w x = true := by
    intro w g x hor hgx
    obtain ⟨hnw, hmem⟩ := hor
    obtain ⟨k, rfl⟩ : ∃ k, w = n + k := ⟨w - n, by omega⟩
    rw [show n + k - n = k by omega] at hmem
    have empty_inv : ∀ k < ([] : List (List ℕ)).length,
        ∀ g ∈ ([] : List (List ℕ)).getD k [],
          g < ([] : List (List ℕ)).length ∧
            ((List.ofFn x).getD (n + g) false = true →
              (List.ofFn x).getD (n + k) false = true) := by
      intro k hk
      rw [List.length_nil] at hk
      exact (Nat.not_lt_zero k hk).elim
    have hinv_all := orReaches_inv C.gates (List.ofFn x) []
      (by rw [List.length_ofFn, List.length_nil, Nat.add_zero]) empty_inv
    have hlen_or : (orSets n C).length = C.gates.length := by
      have h1 := hinv_all.1
      have h2 := foldl_len C.gates (List.ofFn x)
      rw [List.length_ofFn] at h2
      change (C.gates.foldl step (List.ofFn x)).length = n + (orSets n C).length at h1
      change (C.gates.foldl (orStep n) []).length = C.gates.length
      omega
    by_cases hk : k < C.gates.length
    · have hk' : k < (orSets n C).length := by omega
      exact (hinv_all.2 k hk' g hmem).2 hgx
    · have hnone : (orSets n C).getD k [] = [] := by
        rw [List.getD_eq_getElem?_getD, List.getElem?_eq_none (by omega)]
        rfl
      rw [hnone] at hmem
      exact (List.not_mem_nil hmem).elim
  have top_and_true : ∀ (a : ℕ) (x : Fin n → Bool),
      a ∈ tops G C →
      wireVal n C (opnd C a 0) x = true →
      wireVal n C (opnd C a 1) x = true →
      graphFun G ∅ x = true := by
    intro a x ha h0 h1
    obtain ⟨halen_m, haop, habel⟩ := Finset.mem_filter.mp
      (ha : a ∈ (Finset.range C.gates.length).filter
        (fun j => (gateAt C j).op = GateOp.and ∧ BelowF G C j))
    have halen : a < C.gates.length := Finset.mem_range.mp halen_m
    apply habel
    show (C.wires (List.ofFn x)).getD (n + a) false = true
    have hcw : C.wires (List.ofFn x) = C.gates.foldl step (List.ofFn x) := rfl
    rw [hcw]
    have hlenx : (List.ofFn x).length = n := List.length_ofFn
    have hgate := foldl_getD_gate C.gates (List.ofFn x) a halen
    rw [hlenx] at hgate
    rw [hgate]
    have hge : gateAt C a = C.gates[a] := by
      change C.gates.getD a ⟨GateOp.or, []⟩ = C.gates[a]
      rw [List.getD_eq_getElem?_getD, List.getElem?_eq_getElem halen]
      rfl
    have hga : C.gates[a] ∈ C.gates := List.getElem_mem halen
    obtain ⟨p, q, hpq⟩ := List.length_eq_two.mp (hlen2 _ hga)
    have ep : opnd C a 0 = p := by
      change (gateAt C a).inputs.getD 0 0 = p
      rw [hge, hpq]
      rfl
    have eq : opnd C a 1 = q := by
      change (gateAt C a).inputs.getD 1 0 = q
      rw [hge, hpq]
      rfl
    have hp_lt : p < n + a := hRB a halen p (by rw [hge, hpq]; exact List.mem_cons_self)
    have hq_lt : q < n + a := hRB a halen q
      (by rw [hge, hpq]; exact List.mem_cons_of_mem p List.mem_cons_self)
    rw [← hge, haop, hge, hpq]
    change (if p < n + a then (C.gates.foldl step (List.ofFn x)).getD p false else false) &&
      ((if q < n + a then (C.gates.foldl step (List.ofFn x)).getD q false else false) && true) = true
    rw [Bool.and_true, ite_eq_left_of_eq_true _ _ (eq_true hp_lt),
      ite_eq_left_of_eq_true _ _ (eq_true hq_lt), ← ep, ← eq]
    exact Bool.and_eq_true_iff.mpr ⟨h0, h1⟩
  have no_self_graphFun : ∀ u : Fin n, graphFun G ∅ (pairInput u u) = false := by
    intro u
    rw [Bool.eq_false_iff]
    intro h
    change (decide (∃ a ∈ (∅ : Finset (Fin n)), pairInput u u a = true) ||
      decide (∃ a b : Fin n, G.Adj a b ∧ pairInput u u a = true ∧ pairInput u u b = true)) = true at h
    rw [Bool.or_eq_true, decide_eq_true_eq, decide_eq_true_eq] at h
    rcases h with ⟨a, ha, _⟩ | ⟨a, b, hab, ha, hb⟩
    · exact (Finset.notMem_empty a ha).elim
    · change decide (a = u ∨ a = u) = true at ha
      change decide (b = u ∨ b = u) = true at hb
      rw [decide_eq_true_eq] at ha hb
      rcases ha with rfl | rfl <;> rcases hb with rfl | rfl <;> exact G.irrefl hab
  have wmax_from_left : ∀ (a g h : ℕ) (u : Fin n),
      a ∈ tops G C → OrReaches n C (opnd C a 0) g → OrReaches n C (opnd C a 1) h →
      gateVal n C g (pairInput u u) = true → u ∈ wmax G C h := by
    intro a g h u ha horg horh hgu
    refine (Finset.mem_filter.mpr ⟨Finset.mem_univ u, ?_⟩ :
      u ∈ Finset.univ.filter (fun w => ∀ x : Fin n → Bool,
        gateVal n C h x = true → x w = true → graphFun G ∅ x = true))
    intro x hhx hxu
    have hmono : ∀ i : Fin n, pairInput u u i = true → x i = true := by
      intro i hi
      change decide (i = u ∨ i = u) = true at hi
      rw [decide_eq_true_eq] at hi
      rcases hi with rfl | rfl <;> exact hxu
    have hgx := gateVal_mono g (pairInput u u) x hmono hgu
    exact top_and_true a x ha (orReaches_up (opnd C a 0) g x horg hgx)
      (orReaches_up (opnd C a 1) h x horh hhx)
  have wmax_from_right : ∀ (a g h : ℕ) (u : Fin n),
      a ∈ tops G C → OrReaches n C (opnd C a 0) g → OrReaches n C (opnd C a 1) h →
      gateVal n C h (pairInput u u) = true → u ∈ wmax G C g := by
    intro a g h u ha horg horh hhu
    refine (Finset.mem_filter.mpr ⟨Finset.mem_univ u, ?_⟩ :
      u ∈ Finset.univ.filter (fun w => ∀ x : Fin n → Bool,
        gateVal n C g x = true → x w = true → graphFun G ∅ x = true))
    intro x hgx hxu
    have hmono : ∀ i : Fin n, pairInput u u i = true → x i = true := by
      intro i hi
      change decide (i = u ∨ i = u) = true at hi
      rw [decide_eq_true_eq] at hi
      rcases hi with rfl | rfl <;> exact hxu
    have hhx := gateVal_mono h (pairInput u u) x hmono hhu
    exact top_and_true a x ha (orReaches_up (opnd C a 0) g x horg hgx)
      (orReaches_up (opnd C a 1) h x horh hhx)
  have pair_comm : ∀ u v : Fin n, pairInput u v = pairInput v u := by
    intro u v
    funext w
    change decide (w = u ∨ w = v) = decide (w = v ∨ w = u)
    exact decide_eq_decide.mpr or_comm
  classical
  let S := {x9 ∈ Ustar G C |
    (¬∃ (x10 : Fin n) (x11 : Fin n), x9 = s(x10, x11) ∧ ∃ x12 ∈ tops G C, ∃ (x13 : Fin 2),
      ∃ x14 ∈ junk G C, x10 ∈ side C x12 x13 ∧
        OrReaches n C (opnd C x12 ((1 : Fin 2) - x13)) x14 ∧
        gateVal n C x14 (pairInput x10 x11) = true) ∧
    ∃ (x10 : Fin n) (x11 : Fin n), x9 = s(x10, x11) ∧ ∃ x12 ∈ tops G C,
      ∃ x13 ∈ junk G C, ∃ x14 ∈ junk G C, x13 ≠ x14 ∧
        OrReaches n C (opnd C x12 0) x13 ∧ OrReaches n C (opnd C x12 1) x14 ∧
        gateVal n C x13 (pairInput x10 x11) = true ∧
        gateVal n C x14 (pairInput x10 x11) = true}
  let S1 := (Ustar G C).filter (fun e => ∃ u v : Fin n, e = s(u, v) ∧
    ∃ a ∈ tops G C,
      wireVal n C (opnd C a 0) (pairInput u u) = true ∧
      wireVal n C (opnd C a 1) (pairInput v v) = true)
  let S2 := (Ustar G C).filter (fun e => ∃ u v : Fin n, e = s(u, v) ∧
    ∃ g ∈ junk G C, u ∈ wmax G C g ∧
      gateVal n C g (pairInput u v) = true ∧
      gateVal n C g (pairInput u u) = false ∧
      gateVal n C g (pairInput v v) = false)
  let S3 := (Ustar G C).filter (fun e => ∃ u v : Fin n, e = s(u, v) ∧
    ∃ a ∈ tops G C, ∃ g ∈ junk G C, ∃ h ∈ junk G C, g ≠ h ∧
      OrReaches n C (opnd C a 0) g ∧ OrReaches n C (opnd C a 1) h ∧
      gateVal n C g (pairInput u v) = true ∧ gateVal n C h (pairInput u v) = true ∧
      gateVal n C g (pairInput u u) = false ∧ gateVal n C g (pairInput v v) = false ∧
      gateVal n C h (pairInput u u) = false ∧ gateVal n C h (pairInput v v) = false)
  have hsub : S ⊆ S1 ∪ S2 ∪ S3 := by
    intro e he
    obtain ⟨heU, -, u, v, rfl, a, ha, g, hg, h, hh, hne, horg, horh, hguv, hhuv⟩ :=
      Finset.mem_filter.mp he
    have n_uu : ¬ (gateVal n C g (pairInput u u) = true ∧ gateVal n C h (pairInput u u) = true) := by
      rintro ⟨hgu, hhu⟩
      have hgf := top_and_true a (pairInput u u) ha
        (orReaches_up (opnd C a 0) g (pairInput u u) horg hgu)
        (orReaches_up (opnd C a 1) h (pairInput u u) horh hhu)
      rw [no_self_graphFun u] at hgf
      cases hgf
    have n_vv : ¬ (gateVal n C g (pairInput v v) = true ∧ gateVal n C h (pairInput v v) = true) := by
      rintro ⟨hgv, hhv⟩
      have hgf := top_and_true a (pairInput v v) ha
        (orReaches_up (opnd C a 0) g (pairInput v v) horg hgv)
        (orReaches_up (opnd C a 1) h (pairInput v v) horh hhv)
      rw [no_self_graphFun v] at hgf
      cases hgf
    by_cases hgu : gateVal n C g (pairInput u u) = true
    · by_cases hhv : gateVal n C h (pairInput v v) = true
      · exact Finset.mem_union_left _ (Finset.mem_union_left _ (Finset.mem_filter.mpr
          ⟨heU, u, v, rfl, a, ha,
            orReaches_up (opnd C a 0) g (pairInput u u) horg hgu,
            orReaches_up (opnd C a 1) h (pairInput v v) horh hhv⟩))
      · have hhu_f : gateVal n C h (pairInput u u) = false :=
          Bool.eq_false_iff.mpr fun hhu => n_uu ⟨hgu, hhu⟩
        have hhv_f : gateVal n C h (pairInput v v) = false := Bool.eq_false_iff.mpr hhv
        exact Finset.mem_union_left _ (Finset.mem_union_right _ (Finset.mem_filter.mpr
          ⟨heU, u, v, rfl, h, hh, wmax_from_left a g h u ha horg horh hgu, hhuv, hhu_f, hhv_f⟩))
    · by_cases hgv : gateVal n C g (pairInput v v) = true
      · by_cases hhu : gateVal n C h (pairInput u u) = true
        · exact Finset.mem_union_left _ (Finset.mem_union_left _ (Finset.mem_filter.mpr
            ⟨heU, v, u, Sym2.eq_swap, a, ha,
              orReaches_up (opnd C a 0) g (pairInput v v) horg hgv,
              orReaches_up (opnd C a 1) h (pairInput u u) horh hhu⟩))
        · have hhu_f : gateVal n C h (pairInput u u) = false := Bool.eq_false_iff.mpr hhu
          have hhv_f : gateVal n C h (pairInput v v) = false :=
            Bool.eq_false_iff.mpr fun hhv => n_vv ⟨hgv, hhv⟩
          exact Finset.mem_union_left _ (Finset.mem_union_right _ (Finset.mem_filter.mpr
            ⟨heU, v, u, Sym2.eq_swap, h, hh, wmax_from_left a g h v ha horg horh hgv,
              by rw [pair_comm]; exact hhuv, hhv_f, hhu_f⟩))
      · have hgu_f : gateVal n C g (pairInput u u) = false := Bool.eq_false_iff.mpr hgu
        have hgv_f : gateVal n C g (pairInput v v) = false := Bool.eq_false_iff.mpr hgv
        by_cases hhu : gateVal n C h (pairInput u u) = true
        · exact Finset.mem_union_left _ (Finset.mem_union_right _ (Finset.mem_filter.mpr
            ⟨heU, u, v, rfl, g, hg, wmax_from_right a g h u ha horg horh hhu, hguv, hgu_f, hgv_f⟩))
        · by_cases hhv : gateVal n C h (pairInput v v) = true
          · exact Finset.mem_union_left _ (Finset.mem_union_right _ (Finset.mem_filter.mpr
              ⟨heU, v, u, Sym2.eq_swap, g, hg, wmax_from_right a g h v ha horg horh hhv,
                by rw [pair_comm]; exact hguv, hgv_f, hgu_f⟩))
          · have hhu_f : gateVal n C h (pairInput u u) = false := Bool.eq_false_iff.mpr hhu
            have hhv_f : gateVal n C h (pairInput v v) = false := Bool.eq_false_iff.mpr hhv
            exact Finset.mem_union_right _ (Finset.mem_filter.mpr
              ⟨heU, u, v, rfl, a, ha, g, hg, h, hh, hne, horg, horh,
                hguv, hhuv, hgu_f, hgv_f, hhu_f, hhv_f⟩)
  have hcard : S.card ≤ S1.card + S2.card + S3.card :=
    (Finset.card_le_card hsub).trans ((Finset.card_union_le (S1 ∪ S2) S3).trans
      (Nat.add_le_add_right (Finset.card_union_le S1 S2) S3.card))
  change S.card ≤ (k1 + k2 + k3) * (C.size + 1)
  have hs1 : S1.card ≤ k1 * (C.size + 1) := h1
  have hs2 : S2.card ≤ k2 * (C.size + 1) := h2
  have hs3 : S3.card ≤ k3 * (C.size + 1) := h3
  have hsum : S1.card + S2.card + S3.card ≤
      k1 * (C.size + 1) + k2 * (C.size + 1) + k3 * (C.size + 1) :=
    Nat.add_le_add (Nat.add_le_add hs1 hs2) hs3
  rw [Nat.add_mul, Nat.add_mul]
  exact hcard.trans hsum


lean/PEqualNP/Outside/L_meta_1490.lean

theorem PEqualNP.Outside.L_meta_1490 : ∀ (n : ℕ) (G : SimpleGraph (Fin n)) [DecidableRel G.Adj] (C : PEqualNP.CircuitLowerBounds.Circuit),
  PEqualNP.TopForm.NoFourCycle G → G.edgeFinset.Nonempty → PEqualNP.TopForm.IsLeastAO G C →
  PEqualNP.TopForm.TopForm G C →
    (∀ a ∈ PEqualNP.TopForm.tops G C,
      Disjoint (PEqualNP.TopForm.side (n := n) C a 0) (PEqualNP.TopForm.side (n := n) C a 1) ∧
      (∀ u ∈ PEqualNP.TopForm.side (n := n) C a 0, ∀ v ∈ PEqualNP.TopForm.side (n := n) C a 1, G.Adj u v) ∧
      ((PEqualNP.TopForm.side (n := n) C a 0).card ≤ 1 ∨ (PEqualNP.TopForm.side (n := n) C a 1).card ≤ 1)) ∧
    ∀ u v : Fin n, s(u, v) ∈ PEqualNP.TopForm.Ustar G C →
      ∃ a ∈ PEqualNP.TopForm.tops G C, ∃ b : Fin 2, ∃ h ∈ PEqualNP.TopForm.junk G C,
        PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.gateVal n C a (PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.pairInput u v) = true ∧
        PEqualNP.CleanedNonEdge.OrReaches n C (PEqualNP.TopForm.opnd C a b) h ∧
        PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.gateVal n C h (PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.pairInput u v) = true := by
  intro n G _ C h4 _ hL hT
  exact ⟨fun a ha => PEqualNP.Outside.L_meta_1490.top_facts G C h4 hL a ha,
    fun u v huv => PEqualNP.Outside.L_meta_1490.off_star G C hL hT u v huv⟩


lean/PEqualNP/Outside/L_meta_1528.lean

theorem PEqualNP.Outside.L_meta_1528 : ∀ (n : ℕ) (G : SimpleGraph (Fin n)) [DecidableRel G.Adj] (C : PEqualNP.CircuitLowerBounds.Circuit),
  PEqualNP.TopForm.NoFourCycle G → G.edgeFinset.Nonempty → PEqualNP.TopForm.IsLeastAO G C →
  PEqualNP.TopForm.TopForm G C → ∀ (g : ℕ), PEqualNP.TopForm.junk G C = {g} →
    ∃ K : Finset (Fin n), (∀ e ∈ PEqualNP.TopForm.starEdges G C, ∃ v ∈ K, v ∈ e) ∧
      PEqualNP.TopForm.formU G C g ⊆ K ∧ PEqualNP.TopForm.formZ G C g ⊆ K ∧
      G.edgeFinset.card + K.card + (PEqualNP.TopForm.pen G C g K).card + 1 +
        (PEqualNP.TopForm.mixing G C).card + 2 * (PEqualNP.TopForm.centreless G C).card +
        (PEqualNP.TopForm.coneGates G C g).card ≤
      C.size + (PEqualNP.TopForm.Ustar G C).card + 1 := by
  intro n G _ C h4 hE hL hT g hg
  obtain ⟨K, hcov, hU, hZ, hK⟩ := PEqualNP.Outside.L_meta_1528.prev2 n G C h4 hE hL hT g hg
  have h1 := (PEqualNP.Outside.L_meta_1528.prev1 n G C h4 hE hL hT).1
  have hsub1 : PEqualNP.TopForm.mixing G C ⊆ PEqualNP.TopForm.orN G C \ PEqualNP.TopForm.lam G C := by
    intro m hm
    simp only [PEqualNP.TopForm.mixing, Finset.mem_filter] at hm
    refine Finset.mem_sdiff.mpr ⟨hm.1, fun hl => ?_⟩
    simp only [PEqualNP.TopForm.lam, Finset.mem_filter] at hl
    apply hm.2
    rw [Finset.nonempty_iff_ne_empty, Finset.nonempty_iff_ne_empty] at hl
    simp only [hl.2.1, hl.2.2.1]
  have hsub2 : PEqualNP.TopForm.coneGates G C g ⊆
      (PEqualNP.TopForm.orN G C \ PEqualNP.TopForm.lam G C) \ PEqualNP.TopForm.mixing G C :=
    Finset.filter_subset _ _
  have hc2 := Finset.card_le_card hsub2
  have hc3 := Finset.card_sdiff_add_card_eq_card hsub1
  have hj : (PEqualNP.TopForm.junk G C).card = 1 := by rw [hg]; rfl
  have hpen : (PEqualNP.TopForm.pen G C g K).card ≤
      ((Finset.univ : Finset (Fin n)).filter (fun u => (∃ a ∈ PEqualNP.TopForm.tops G C, ∃ b : Fin 2,
          PEqualNP.TopForm.side (n := n) C a b = {u} ∧
          PEqualNP.CleanedNonEdge.OrReaches n C (PEqualNP.TopForm.opnd C a b) g) ∧
          ∃ v, G.Adj u v ∧ s(u, v) ∉ PEqualNP.TopForm.Ustar G C ∧ v ∉ PEqualNP.TopForm.wmax G C g ∧ v ∉ K)).card := by
    apply Finset.card_le_card
    intro u hu
    simp only [PEqualNP.TopForm.pen, PEqualNP.TopForm.formU, Finset.mem_filter, Finset.mem_univ, true_and] at hu ⊢
    exact hu
  refine ⟨K, hcov, ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
  · intro u hu
    apply hU
    simpa only [PEqualNP.TopForm.formU] using hu
  · intro w hw
    apply hZ
    simpa only [PEqualNP.TopForm.formZ] using hw
  · omega


lean/PEqualNP/Outside/L_meta_4322.lean

theorem PEqualNP.Outside.L_meta_4322 : ∀ (x1 : ℕ) (x2 : SimpleGraph (Fin x1)) [x3 : DecidableRel x2.Adj] (x4 : PEqualNP.CircuitLowerBounds.Circuit), (∀ (x5 : Fin x1) (x6 : x2.Walk x5 x5), x6.IsCycle → (5 : ℕ) ≤ x6.length) → PEqualNP.TopForm.IsAO2 x1 x4 → {x7 ∈ x2.edgeFinset | ∃ (x8 : Fin x1) (x9 : Fin x1), x7 = s(x8, x9) ∧ ∃ x10 ∈ PEqualNP.TopForm.tops x2 x4, ∃ (x11 : Fin (2 : ℕ)), ∃ x12 ∈ PEqualNP.TopForm.junk x2 x4, x8 ∈ PEqualNP.TopForm.side x4 x10 x11 ∧ PEqualNP.CleanedNonEdge.OrReaches x1 x4 (PEqualNP.TopForm.opnd x4 x10 ((1 : Fin (2 : ℕ)) - x11)) x12 ∧ (x8 ∈ PEqualNP.TopForm.side x4 x12 (0 : Fin (2 : ℕ)) ∨ x9 ∈ PEqualNP.TopForm.side x4 x12 (0 : Fin (2 : ℕ))) ∧ (x8 ∈ PEqualNP.TopForm.side x4 x12 (1 : Fin (2 : ℕ)) ∨ x9 ∈ PEqualNP.TopForm.side x4 x12 (1 : Fin (2 : ℕ)))}.card ≤ (27 : ℕ) * x4.size := by
  intro n G _ C hg hC
  have h4 : PEqualNP.Outside.L_meta_4322.C4free G := by
    intro a b x y hab hxy hax hay hbx hby
    exact PEqualNP.Outside.L_meta_4322.no4 G hg a x b y hax hbx.symm hby hay.symm hab hxy
  apply PEqualNP.Outside.L_meta_4322.count h4
  intro e he
  rw [Finset.mem_filter] at he
  obtain ⟨hedge, u, v, rfl, a, hat, b, j, hjj, hu, hr, h0, h1⟩ := he
  have hadj : G.Adj u v := by simpa using hedge
  simp only [PEqualNP.TopForm.tops, PEqualNP.TopForm.gatesWhere, Finset.mem_filter,
    Finset.mem_range] at hat
  simp only [PEqualNP.TopForm.junk, PEqualNP.TopForm.gatesWhere, Finset.mem_filter,
    Finset.mem_range] at hjj
  exact PEqualNP.Outside.L_meta_4322.mainCase h4 hC hat.1 hat.2.1 b
    (fun u' hu' => PEqualNP.Outside.L_meta_4322.top_ctx G hC hat.1 hat.2.1 hat.2.2 b hu')
    hjj.1 hjj.2.1 hr hadj hu h0 h1

theorem PEqualNP.Outside.R_meta_1597_2 :
    (∀ (G : SimpleGraph (Fin 11)) [DecidableRel G.Adj] (D : PEqualNP.CircuitLowerBounds.Circuit),
       G = PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.edgeGraph 11 [(0, 9), (0, 10), (1, 4), (1, 8), (2, 5), (2, 10), (3, 6), (3, 7), (4, 8), (4, 10), (5, 8), (5, 9), (6, 7), (6, 10), (7, 9), (8, 9)] →
       PEqualNP.TopForm.IsLeastAO G D → PEqualNP.TopForm.TopForm G D →
       let T : Finset ℕ := PEqualNP.TopForm.tops G D
       let C0 : Finset ℕ := PEqualNP.TopForm.centreless G D
       let J : Finset ℕ := PEqualNP.TopForm.junk G D
       let U : Finset (Sym2 (Fin 11)) := PEqualNP.TopForm.Ustar G D

         let S : Fin 2 → Finset (Fin 11) := PEqualNP.TopForm.side D a

       15 + (T \ C0).card + 2 * C0.card + J.card + (PEqualNP.TopForm.orN G D \ PEqualNP.TopForm.lam G D).card ≤ D.size + U.card →
       (∀ K : Finset (Fin 11), PEqualNP.MetaComplexity.NestedCover.IsMinCover (G.deleteEdges ↑U) K → K.card ≤ (T \ C0).card) →
       3 ≤ J.card → 22 ≤ D.size) →
    (∀ (G : SimpleGraph (Fin 11)) [DecidableRel G.Adj] (D : PEqualNP.CircuitLowerBounds.Circuit) (g1 g2 : ℕ),
       G = PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.edgeGraph 11 [(0, 9), (0, 10), (1, 4), (1, 8), (2, 5), (2, 10), (3, 6), (3, 7), (4, 8), (4, 10), (5, 8), (5, 9), (6, 7), (6, 10), (7, 9), (8, 9)] →
       PEqualNP.TopForm.IsLeastAO G D → PEqualNP.TopForm.TopForm G D →
       let T : Finset ℕ := PEqualNP.TopForm.tops G D
       let C0 : Finset ℕ := PEqualNP.TopForm.centreless G D
       let U : Finset (Sym2 (Fin 11)) := PEqualNP.TopForm.Ustar G D
       (∀ a ∈ T,
         let S : Fin 2 → Finset (Fin 11) := PEqualNP.TopForm.side D a
         (∀ u ∈ S 0, ∀ v ∈ S 1, G.Adj u v) ∧ ((S 0).card ≤ 1 ∨ (S 1).card ≤ 1)) →
       (∀ u v : Fin 11, s(u, v) ∈ U → ∃ a ∈ T, ∃ b : Fin 2, ∃ h : ℕ, (h = g1 ∨ h = g2) ∧ PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.gateVal 11 D a (PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.pairInput u v) = true ∧ PEqualNP.CleanedNonEdge.OrReaches 11 D (PEqualNP.TopForm.opnd D a b) h ∧ PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.gateVal 11 D h (PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.pairInput u v) = true) →
       17 + (T \ C0).card + 2 * C0.card + (PEqualNP.TopForm.orN G D \ PEqualNP.TopForm.lam G D).card ≤ D.size + U.card →
       (∀ K : Finset (Fin 11), PEqualNP.MetaComplexity.NestedCover.IsMinCover (G.deleteEdges ↑U) K → K.card ≤ (T \ C0).card) →
       PEqualNP.TopForm.junk G D = {g1, g2} → PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.InCone 11 D g1 g2 → 22 ≤ D.size) →
    (∀ (G : SimpleGraph (Fin 11)) [DecidableRel G.Adj] (D : PEqualNP.CircuitLowerBounds.Circuit) (g1 g2 : ℕ),
       G = PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.edgeGraph 11 [(0, 9), (0, 10), (1, 4), (1, 8), (2, 5), (2, 10), (3, 6), (3, 7), (4, 8), (4, 10), (5, 8), (5, 9), (6, 7), (6, 10), (7, 9), (8, 9)] →
       PEqualNP.TopForm.IsLeastAO G D → PEqualNP.TopForm.TopForm G D →
       let T : Finset ℕ := PEqualNP.TopForm.tops G D
       let C0 : Finset ℕ := PEqualNP.TopForm.centreless G D
       let U : Finset (Sym2 (Fin 11)) := PEqualNP.TopForm.Ustar G D
       let ic : ℕ → ℕ → Prop := PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.InCone 11 D
       (∀ a ∈ T,
         let S : Fin 2 → Finset (Fin 11) := PEqualNP.TopForm.side D a
         (∀ u ∈ S 0, ∀ v ∈ S 1, G.Adj u v) ∧ ((S 0).card ≤ 1 ∨ (S 1).card ≤ 1)) →
       (∀ u v : Fin 11, s(u, v) ∈ U → ∃ a ∈ T, ∃ b : Fin 2, ∃ h : ℕ, (h = g1 ∨ h = g2) ∧ PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.gateVal 11 D a (PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.pairInput u v) = true ∧ PEqualNP.CleanedNonEdge.OrReaches 11 D (PEqualNP.TopForm.opnd D a b) h ∧ PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.gateVal 11 D h (PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.pairInput u v) = true) →
       17 + (T \ C0).card + 2 * C0.card + (PEqualNP.TopForm.orN G D \ PEqualNP.TopForm.lam G D).card ≤ D.size + U.card →
       (∀ K : Finset (Fin 11), PEqualNP.MetaComplexity.NestedCover.IsMinCover (G.deleteEdges ↑U) K → K.card ≤ (T \ C0).card) →
       g1 < g2 → PEqualNP.TopForm.junk G D = {g1, g2} → ¬ ic g1 g2 → ¬ ic g2 g1 → (∃ a ∈ T, ic g1 a ∧ ic g2 a) → 22 ≤ D.size) →
    (∀ (G : SimpleGraph (Fin 11)) [DecidableRel G.Adj] (D : PEqualNP.CircuitLowerBounds.Circuit) (g1 g2 : ℕ),
       G = PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.edgeGraph 11 [(0, 9), (0, 10), (1, 4), (1, 8), (2, 5), (2, 10), (3, 6), (3, 7), (4, 8), (4, 10), (5, 8), (5, 9), (6, 7), (6, 10), (7, 9), (8, 9)] →
       PEqualNP.TopForm.IsLeastAO G D → PEqualNP.TopForm.TopForm G D →
       let T : Finset ℕ := PEqualNP.TopForm.tops G D
       let C0 : Finset ℕ := PEqualNP.TopForm.centreless G D
       let U : Finset (Sym2 (Fin 11)) := PEqualNP.TopForm.Ustar G D
       let ic : ℕ → ℕ → Prop := PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.InCone 11 D
       (∀ a ∈ T,
         let S : Fin 2 → Finset (Fin 11) := PEqualNP.TopForm.side D a
         (∀ u ∈ S 0, ∀ v ∈ S 1, G.Adj u v) ∧ ((S 0).card ≤ 1 ∨ (S 1).card ≤ 1)) →
       (∀ u v : Fin 11, s(u, v) ∈ U → ∃ a ∈ T, ∃ b : Fin 2, ∃ h : ℕ, (h = g1 ∨ h = g2) ∧ PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.gateVal 11 D a (PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.pairInput u v) = true ∧ PEqualNP.CleanedNonEdge.OrReaches 11 D (PEqualNP.TopForm.opnd D a b) h ∧ PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.gateVal 11 D h (PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.pairInput u v) = true) →
       17 + (T \ C0).card + 2 * C0.card + (PEqualNP.TopForm.orN G D \ PEqualNP.TopForm.lam G D).card ≤ D.size + U.card →
       (∀ K : Finset (Fin 11), PEqualNP.MetaComplexity.NestedCover.IsMinCover (G.deleteEdges ↑U) K → K.card ≤ (T \ C0).card) →
       g1 < g2 → PEqualNP.TopForm.junk G D = {g1, g2} → ¬ ic g1 g2 → ¬ ic g2 g1 → (∀ a ∈ T, ic g1 a → ¬ ic g2 a) → 22 ≤ D.size) →
    (∀ (n : ℕ) (G : SimpleGraph (Fin n)) [DecidableRel G.Adj] (C : PEqualNP.CircuitLowerBounds.Circuit), PEqualNP.TopForm.NoFourCycle G → G.edgeFinset.Nonempty → PEqualNP.TopForm.IsLeastAO G C → PEqualNP.TopForm.TopForm G C → (∀ a ∈ PEqualNP.TopForm.tops G C, Disjoint (PEqualNP.TopForm.side (n := n) C a 0) (PEqualNP.TopForm.side (n := n) C a 1) ∧ (∀ u ∈ PEqualNP.TopForm.side (n := n) C a 0, ∀ v ∈ PEqualNP.TopForm.side (n := n) C a 1, G.Adj u v) ∧ ((PEqualNP.TopForm.side (n := n) C a 0).card ≤ 1 ∨ (PEqualNP.TopForm.side (n := n) C a 1).card ≤ 1)) ∧ ∀ u v : Fin n, s(u, v) ∈ PEqualNP.TopForm.Ustar G C → ∃ a ∈ PEqualNP.TopForm.tops G C, ∃ b : Fin 2, ∃ h ∈ PEqualNP.TopForm.junk G C, PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.gateVal n C a (PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.pairInput u v) = true ∧ PEqualNP.CleanedNonEdge.OrReaches n C (PEqualNP.TopForm.opnd C a b) h ∧ PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.gateVal n C h (PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.pairInput u v) = true) → (∀ (n : ℕ) (G : SimpleGraph (Fin n)) [DecidableRel G.Adj] (C : PEqualNP.CircuitLowerBounds.Circuit), PEqualNP.TopForm.NoFourCycle G → G.edgeFinset.Nonempty → PEqualNP.TopForm.IsLeastAO G C → PEqualNP.TopForm.TopForm G C → G.edgeFinset.card + (PEqualNP.TopForm.tops G C \ PEqualNP.TopForm.centreless G C).card + 2 * (PEqualNP.TopForm.centreless G C).card + (PEqualNP.TopForm.junk G C).card + (PEqualNP.TopForm.orN G C \ PEqualNP.TopForm.lam G C).card ≤ C.size + (PEqualNP.TopForm.Ustar G C).card + 1 ∧ ∀ K : Finset (Fin n), PEqualNP.MetaComplexity.NestedCover.IsMinCover (G.deleteEdges ↑(PEqualNP.TopForm.Ustar G C)) K → K.card ≤ (PEqualNP.TopForm.tops G C \ PEqualNP.TopForm.centreless G C).card) → ∀ D : PEqualNP.CircuitLowerBounds.Circuit, PEqualNP.TopForm.IsLeastAO (PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.edgeGraph 11 [(0, 9), (0, 10), (1, 4), (1, 8), (2, 5), (2, 10), (3, 6), (3, 7), (4, 8), (4, 10), (5, 8), (5, 9), (6, 7), (6, 10), (7, 9), (8, 9)]) D → PEqualNP.TopForm.TopForm (PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.edgeGraph 11 [(0, 9), (0, 10), (1, 4), (1, 8), (2, 5), (2, 10), (3, 6), (3, 7), (4, 8), (4, 10), (5, 8), (5, 9), (6, 7), (6, 10), (7, 9), (8, 9)]) D → 2 ≤ (PEqualNP.TopForm.junk (PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.edgeGraph 11 [(0, 9), (0, 10), (1, 4), (1, 8), (2, 5), (2, 10), (3, 6), (3, 7), (4, 8), (4, 10), (5, 8), (5, 9), (6, 7), (6, 10), (7, 9), (8, 9)]) D).card → 22 ≤ D.size := by
  intro c1 c2 c3 c4 h1 h2 D hL hT hj2
  set G0 := PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.edgeGraph 11 [(0, 9), (0, 10), (1, 4), (1, 8), (2, 5), (2, 10), (3, 6), (3, 7), (4, 8), (4, 10), (5, 8), (5, 9), (6, 7), (6, 10), (7, 9), (8, 9)]
  change 2 ≤ (PEqualNP.TopForm.junk G0 D).card at hj2
  have hnf : PEqualNP.TopForm.NoFourCycle G0 := by decide
  have hne : G0.edgeFinset.Nonempty := by decide
  have hecard : G0.edgeFinset.card = 16 := by decide
  obtain ⟨hstar0, hoff⟩ := h1 11 G0 D hnf hne hL hT
  obtain ⟨hcnt, hcov⟩ := h2 11 G0 D hnf hne hL hT
  rw [hecard] at hcnt
  have hstar : ∀ a ∈ PEqualNP.TopForm.tops G0 D,
      let S : Fin 2 → Finset (Fin 11) := PEqualNP.TopForm.side D a
      (∀ u ∈ S 0, ∀ v ∈ S 1, G0.Adj u v) ∧ ((S 0).card ≤ 1 ∨ (S 1).card ≤ 1) := by
    intro a ha
    exact (hstar0 a ha).2
  by_cases hj3 : 3 ≤ (PEqualNP.TopForm.junk G0 D).card
  · have hcnt1 : 15 + (PEqualNP.TopForm.tops G0 D \ PEqualNP.TopForm.centreless G0 D).card +
        2 * (PEqualNP.TopForm.centreless G0 D).card + (PEqualNP.TopForm.junk G0 D).card +
        (PEqualNP.TopForm.orN G0 D \ PEqualNP.TopForm.lam G0 D).card ≤
        D.size + (PEqualNP.TopForm.Ustar G0 D).card := by omega
    exact c1 G0 D rfl hL hT hstar hoff hcnt1 hcov hj3
  · have hj_eq2 : (PEqualNP.TopForm.junk G0 D).card = 2 := by omega
    have hcnt2 : 17 + (PEqualNP.TopForm.tops G0 D \ PEqualNP.TopForm.centreless G0 D).card +
        2 * (PEqualNP.TopForm.centreless G0 D).card +
        (PEqualNP.TopForm.orN G0 D \ PEqualNP.TopForm.lam G0 D).card ≤
        D.size + (PEqualNP.TopForm.Ustar G0 D).card := by omega
    obtain ⟨x1, x2, hne12, hjset0⟩ := Finset.card_eq_two.mp hj_eq2
    have hord : ∃ g1 g2 : ℕ, g1 < g2 ∧ PEqualNP.TopForm.junk G0 D = {g1, g2} := by
      rcases lt_or_gt_of_ne hne12 with hlt | hlt
      · exact ⟨x1, x2, hlt, hjset0⟩
      · rw [Finset.pair_comm] at hjset0
        exact ⟨x2, x1, hlt, hjset0⟩
    obtain ⟨g1, g2, hlt12, hjset⟩ := hord
    have hoffPair : ∀ (a0 b0 : ℕ), PEqualNP.TopForm.junk G0 D = {a0, b0} →
        ∀ u v : Fin 11, s(u, v) ∈ PEqualNP.TopForm.Ustar G0 D →
          ∃ a ∈ PEqualNP.TopForm.tops G0 D, ∃ b : Fin 2, ∃ h : ℕ, (h = a0 ∨ h = b0) ∧
            PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.gateVal 11 D a (PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.pairInput u v) = true ∧
            PEqualNP.CleanedNonEdge.OrReaches 11 D (PEqualNP.TopForm.opnd D a b) h ∧
            PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.gateVal 11 D h (PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.pairInput u v) = true := by
      intro a0 b0 hjs u v huv
      obtain ⟨a, ha, b, h, hh, hv1, hor, hv2⟩ := hoff u v huv
      rw [hjs] at hh
      simp only [Finset.mem_insert, Finset.mem_singleton] at hh
      exact ⟨a, ha, b, h, hh, hv1, hor, hv2⟩
    by_cases hc12 : PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.InCone 11 D g1 g2
    · exact c2 G0 D g1 g2 rfl hL hT hstar (hoffPair g1 g2 hjset) hcnt2 hcov hjset hc12
    · by_cases hc21 : PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.InCone 11 D g2 g1
      · have hjset' : PEqualNP.TopForm.junk G0 D = {g2, g1} := by rw [Finset.pair_comm]; exact hjset
        exact c2 G0 D g2 g1 rfl hL hT hstar (hoffPair g2 g1 hjset') hcnt2 hcov hjset' hc21
      · by_cases hsh : ∃ a ∈ PEqualNP.TopForm.tops G0 D, PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.InCone 11 D g1 a ∧ PEqualNP.EdgeOwnership.InCone 11 D g2 a
        · exact c3 G0 D g1 g2 rfl hL hT hstar (hoffPair g1 g2 hjset) hcnt2 hcov hlt12 hjset hc12 hc21 hsh
        · refine c4 G0 D g1 g2 rfl hL hT hstar (hoffPair g1 g2 hjset) hcnt2 hcov hlt12 hjset hc12 hc21 ?_
          intro a ha hg1
          exact fun hg2 => hsh ⟨a, ha, hg1, hg2⟩


lean/PEqualNP/Outside/L_meta_1491.lean

theorem PEqualNP.Outside.L_meta_1491 : ∀ (n : ℕ) (G : SimpleGraph (Fin n)) [DecidableRel G.Adj] (C : PEqualNP.CircuitLowerBounds.Circuit),
  PEqualNP.TopForm.NoFourCycle G → G.edgeFinset.Nonempty → PEqualNP.TopForm.IsLeastAO G C →
  PEqualNP.TopForm.TopForm G C →
    G.edgeFinset.card + (PEqualNP.TopForm.tops G C \ PEqualNP.TopForm.centreless G C).card +
        2 * (PEqualNP.TopForm.centreless G C).card + (PEqualNP.TopForm.junk G C).card +
        (PEqualNP.TopForm.orN G C \ PEqualNP.TopForm.lam G C).card ≤
      C.size + (PEqualNP.TopForm.Ustar G C).card + 1 ∧
    ∀ K : Finset (Fin n),
      PEqualNP.MetaComplexity.NestedCover.IsMinCover (G.deleteEdges ↑(PEqualNP.TopForm.Ustar G C)) K →
      K.card ≤ (PEqualNP.TopForm.tops G C \ PEqualNP.TopForm.centreless G C).card := by
  intro n G _ C h4 hne hL hTF
  exact PEqualNP.Outside.L_meta_1491.main n G C h4 hne hL hTF

theorem PEqualNP.Outside.L_meta_4183 : ∀ (x1 : ℕ) (x2 : SimpleGraph (Fin x1)) [x3 : DecidableRel x2.Adj] (x4 : PEqualNP.CircuitLowerBounds.Circuit), PEqualNP.TopForm.NoFourCycle x2 → x2.edgeFinset.Nonempty → PEqualNP.TopForm.IsLeastAO x2 x4 → PEqualNP.TopForm.TopForm x2 x4 → x2.edgeFinset.card + (PEqualNP.TopForm.tops x2 x4 \ PEqualNP.TopForm.centreless x2 x4).card + (2 : ℕ) * (PEqualNP.TopForm.centreless x2 x4).card + (PEqualNP.TopForm.junk x2 x4).card + (PEqualNP.TopForm.orN x2 x4 \ PEqualNP.TopForm.lam x2 x4).card ≤ x4.size + (PEqualNP.TopForm.Ustar x2 x4).card + (1 : ℕ) := by
  intro x1 x2 x3 x4 h4 hne hL hTF
  exact (PEqualNP.Outside.L_meta_4183.main x1 x2 x4 h4 hne hL hTF).1

theorem PEqualNP.Outside.L_meta_2695 : ∀ (x1 : ℕ) (x2 : SimpleGraph (Fin x1)) [x3 : DecidableRel x2.Adj] (x4 : PEqualNP.CircuitLowerBounds.Circuit), (∀ x5 ∈ x4.gates, x5.inputs.length = (2 : ℕ)) → ∀ x6 ∈ PEqualNP.TopForm.tops x2 x4, ∀ (x8 : Fin (2 : ℕ)), ∀ x9 ∈ PEqualNP.TopForm.junk x2 x4, PEqualNP.CleanedNonEdge.OrReaches x1 x4 (PEqualNP.TopForm.opnd x4 x6 x8) x9 → x6 ∈ PEqualNP.TopForm.centreless x2 x4 ∨ (PEqualNP.TopForm.orN x2 x4 \ PEqualNP.TopForm.lam x2 x4).Nonempty :=
  PEqualNP.Outside.L_meta_2695.main

  (∀ u v : Fin n, G.Adj u v → s u ≠ s v) → PEqualNP.TopForm.NoFourCycle G → PEqualNP.TopForm.IsLeastAO G C →
  (PEqualNP.TopForm.junk G C).card ≠ 1 := by
  exact PEqualNP.Outside.L_meta_3760.Close.final_proof
